# Reader at Work 1 — Reading, Listening & Writing Çalışma Kitabı

> Toplam **102** pasaj | Her pasaj için Dinleme (Edge-TTS MP3), Okuma (Kitap + Ekstra Sınav Soruları) ve Yazma (2 Seviyeli Görev + Kelime Listesi) içerir.

---

## Passage 1: Mary

- **ID:** `raw1_001` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** People & Education | **Sayfa:** -
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_001_mary.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Mary Snyder is from ____. *(İpucu: place / state)*

2. Her class is ____, but it is nice. *(İpucu: adjective)*

3. Habib is from ____. *(İpucu: country)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is Mary Snyder's job?

   - A) A university student

   - B) A school principal

   - C) A teacher at METU

   - D) A travel guide

2. What do we learn about Mrs. Snyder's class?

   - A) All of the students are Turkish.

   - B) Students come from several different countries.

   - C) The class is very large.

   - D) Mary Snyder is originally from Turkey.

3. Where is Mohan from?

   - A) India

   - B) Iran

   - C) Egypt

   - D) Turkey


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Every student in Mrs. Snyder's class is from Turkey.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Mary Snyder has been teaching at METU for five years.


### 2. Reading

Mary Snyder is American. She is from Michigan. She is a teacher at METU. Her class is small, but it is nice. The students aren't all from Turkey. Robab is from Iran. Mohan is from India and Habib is from Egypt. Mrs. Snyder is a very good teacher.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension*

1. Is Mrs. Snyder British?

2. Where is she from?

3. Is she a student?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* What can be inferred about Mrs. Snyder's class?

   - A) The class meets only in the evenings.

   - B) All of the students speak the same native language.

   - C) The class includes international students from different countries.

   - D) Mrs. Snyder plans to move back to Michigan soon.

2. *[Detail]* How is Mrs. Snyder's class described in the passage?

   - A) Crowded and noisy

   - B) Small and pleasant

   - C) Difficult and boring

   - D) Large and formal

3. *[Main Idea]* What is the main purpose of this passage?

   - A) To explain the history of METU

   - B) To compare education systems in four countries

   - C) To describe Michigan's weather and culture

   - D) To introduce a teacher and her diverse class


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: Introducing Mrs. Snyder** (50-70 words): Write a short summary introducing Mary Snyder, her job, and her students in your own words.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Class Size and Diversity** (80-110 words): Do you prefer a small class with students from different countries, or a large class? Give two simple reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **teacher** (*ogretmen*): Mary is an English teacher at a university.

- **small** (*kucuk*): Our classroom is small, but it is very comfortable.

- **students** (*ogrenciler*): The students in my class are friendly and helpful.

- **be from** (*...den / ...dan gelmek, ...li olmak*): My friend is from Egypt, and she speaks Arabic.

- **very good** (*cok iyi*): We have a very good teacher this semester.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Metindeki temel bilgileri (isim, ulke, meslek) dogru yazdim mi?

- [ ] 'to be' (am / is / are) fiillerini oznelere uygun kullandim mi?

- [ ] Ozel isimleri (ulke, sehir, kisi adlari) buyuk harfle baslattim mi?

- [ ] Cumlelerin sonuna uygun noktalama isareti koydum mu?

## Passage 2: Hanako

- **ID:** `raw1_002` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Business & Family | **Sayfa:** -
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_002_hanako.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Pop Fun is an international ____ with two offices in Japan. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

2. Mrs. Sato is the first woman to direct the company's ____ office. *(İpucu: city)*

3. Tomiko is a ____ for Japan Airlines in New York City. *(İpucu: job / noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is Hanako Sato's position at Pop Fun?

   - A) She is a student at New York University.

   - B) She works as a secretary for Japan Airlines.

   - C) She is the director of Pop Fun's Tokyo office.

   - D) She owns a restaurant in Tokyo.

2. How old is the Tokyo office of Pop Fun?

   - A) Five years

   - B) Fifteen years

   - C) Nineteen years

   - D) Twenty-four years

3. What does Akiko do?

   - A) She works for Pop Fun in Japan.

   - B) She is a secretary in Tokyo.

   - C) She lives with her parents in Japan.

   - D) She studies at New York University.


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Pop Fun has more than one office in Japan.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Both of Mrs. Sato's daughters live and work in Tokyo.


### 2. Reading

Hanako Sato, a well-known businesswoman, is the new director of Pop Fun, an international record company with two offices in Japan. Mrs. Sato is the first woman to direct the company's Tokyo office in its fifteen-year history. Mrs. Sato is also a successful housewife and mother. She and her husband, Taro Sato, have got two daughters. Tomiko, 24, is a secretary for Japan Airlines in New York City. Akiko, 19, is a student at New York University.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension*

1. What is 'Pop Fun'?

2. In which city does Mrs. Sato work?

3. How old are Tomiko and Akiko?

4. What do they do?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the main idea about Hanako Sato in the passage?

   - A) She has achieved success in both her career and her family life.

   - B) She plans to move to New York to join her daughters.

   - C) She started Pop Fun fifteen years ago by herself.

   - D) She prefers being a housewife to working at a record company.

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In the passage, what does the word 'well-known' mean?

   - A) Very young

   - B) Quite shy

   - C) Famous

   - D) Wealthy

3. *[Inference]* What can be inferred from the fact that Mrs. Sato is 'the first woman to direct the company's Tokyo office'?

   - A) Pop Fun has never hired female employees before.

   - B) Previous directors of the Tokyo office were all men.

   - C) Taro Sato also works at Pop Fun.

   - D) Tomiko will become the next director of Pop Fun.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: Hanako Sato's Life** (60-80 words): Write a short summary about Hanako Sato's job and family using your own words.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Work and Family Balance** (80-110 words): Can someone be successful in both a busy career and family life? Why or why not?


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **well-known** (*taninmis, unlu*): She is a well-known businesswoman in her country.

- **director** (*mudur, yonetici*): Mr. Lee is the new director of our local bank.

- **international** (*uluslararasi*): This is an international company with offices in five countries.

- **successful** (*basarili*): He is a successful doctor at a large hospital.

- **secretary** (*sekreter*): Tomiko works as a secretary in a big office.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Hanako Sato'nun isini ve ailesini acik bir sekilde tanittim mi?

- [ ] Kizlarinin yaslarini ve ne yaptiklarini dogru yazdim mi?

- [ ] 'have got / has got' ve genis zaman fiillerini dogru cekimledim mi?

- [ ] Sehir ve kurum adlarini dogru ve buyuk harfle yazdim mi?

## Passage 3: Longleat House

- **ID:** `raw1_003` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Travel & Landmarks | **Sayfa:** 2
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_003_longleat_house.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Longleat House is ____ kilometres west of London. *(İpucu: number)*

2. Visitors can walk through the rooms and see the ____ inside. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

3. The Safari Park is open only in the ____. *(İpucu: season)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where is Longleat House located?

   - A) 140 kilometres east of London

   - B) Between Salisbury and Bath

   - C) Inside the city of London

   - D) Near the coast north of Bath

2. What can visitors do in the Safari Park?

   - A) Buy antique furniture

   - B) Meet Lord Bath in person

   - C) Drive through and see many animals

   - D) Stay overnight in the rooms

3. For how many hours is Longleat House open each day?

   - A) 8 hours

   - B) 6 hours

   - C) 10 hours

   - D) 12 hours


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Both Longleat House and the Safari Park are open all year round.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Visitors must pay extra to enter the Safari Park.


### 2. Reading

Longleat House is 140 kilometres west of London, between the cities of Salisbury and Bath. The house is the home of Lord Bath. It is about 400 years old. Visitors can walk through and look at the beautiful rooms and they can see the antique furniture in the rooms. There is also a Safari Park at Longleat House. Visitors can drive through the park and see many animals there.

Longleat House is open every day from 10 o'clock in the morning to 6 o'clock in the evening. The Safari Park is open only in the summer.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension*

1. Where is Longleat House?

2. Who owns the house?

3. What can visitors see inside the house?

4. What can visitors do in the Safari Park?

5. When is Longleat House open?

6. When is the Safari Park open?


*Reference*

7. What does 'It' refer to in "It is about 400 years old"?

8. What does 'they' refer to in "and they can see the antique furniture in the rooms"?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of the passage?

   - A) To tell the biography of Lord Bath

   - B) To explain how antique furniture is made

   - C) To warn tourists about wild animals in England

   - D) To give visitor information about a historic house and park

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* Based on the passage, what does the word 'antique' most likely mean?

   - A) Modern and cheap

   - B) Very old

   - C) Broken and useless

   - D) Made of plastic

3. *[Detail]* Which of the following is true according to the passage?

   - A) Longleat House is closed during the winter months.

   - B) Visitors cannot enter the rooms of the house.

   - C) A tourist visiting in January can see the house, but not the Safari Park.

   - D) The Safari Park opens at 6 o'clock in the evening.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: Visiting Longleat House** (60-80 words): Summarize the main facts about Longleat House and its Safari Park in your own words.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Visiting Historic Places** (80-110 words): Would you like to visit a historic place like Longleat House? Explain what interests you most about it.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **antique furniture** (*antika, eski degerli*): We saw a beautiful antique table in the museum.

- **walk through** (*icinden yurumek, gezmek*): Tourists can walk through the old castle gardens.

- **between** (*arasinda*): Our town is located between two big mountains.

- **open every day** (*her gun acik olmak*): The library is open every day except Sunday.

- **drive through** (*aracla icinden gecmek*): Visitors can drive through the park in their own cars.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Evin konumunu, yasini ve acilis saatlerini dogru ozetledim mi?

- [ ] 'can' ve yer-yon edatlarini (between, through, from...to) dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Paragraf duzenine ve cumle baglaclarina dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Yazim ve noktalama hatasi olmadigini kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 4: New York University

- **ID:** `raw1_004` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Campus & City Life | **Sayfa:** 3
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_004_new_york_university.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. There is a ____ on 9th Street. *(İpucu: place / building)*

2. There is a bank on ____, and there are two on 8th Street. *(İpucu: street / avenue name)*

3. The restaurants and coffee shops in Greenwich Village are very ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where is New York University located?

   - A) On 5th Avenue only

   - B) In Greenwich Village

   - C) Outside New York City

   - D) On 9th Street only

2. How many banks are mentioned near the campus?

   - A) One

   - B) Two

   - C) Three

   - D) Four

3. What does the speaker say about the restaurants and coffee shops?

   - A) They are very popular.

   - B) They are too expensive for students.

   - C) They are far from the campus.

   - D) They are only open in the morning.


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Students can easily find most things they need close to the university.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The post office on 9th Street closes at 5 o'clock.


### 2. Reading

New York University is in Greenwich Village. Students can get almost everything they need near the university campus. There is a post office on 9th Street. There is a bank on 5th Avenue, and there are two on 8th Street. The restaurants and coffee shops in Greenwich Village are very popular.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension*

1. Where is New York University?

2. What's there on 9th Street?

3. How many banks are there near the university?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the main idea of the passage?

   - A) The area around New York University is very convenient for students.

   - B) Greenwich Village has too many banks and no restaurants.

   - C) Students at New York University must travel far to buy food.

   - D) There is only one street near New York University.

2. *[Reference]* In the sentence "There is a bank on 5th Avenue, and there are two on 8th Street", what does "two" refer to?

   - A) Two post offices

   - B) Two coffee shops

   - C) Two universities

   - D) Two banks

3. *[Inference]* What can be inferred about life near the New York University campus?

   - A) Most students dislike eating near the campus.

   - B) 9th Street is the longest street in New York.

   - C) Students do not need to go far for daily services and food.

   - D) The university has no campus buildings of its own.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: Around NYU Campus** (50-70 words): Write a short summary describing the area around New York University.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Campus Facilities** (80-110 words): Why is it important for students to have banks, post offices, and cafes near their university campus? Give two reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **university campus** (*universite yerleskesi, kampus*): Students live in dormitories near the university campus.

- **post office** (*postane*): I need to go to the post office to send a letter.

- **coffee shop** (*kahve dukkani, kafe*): There is a small coffee shop next to my school.

- **popular** (*sevilen, populer*): This restaurant is very popular with college students.

- **almost everything** (*neredeyse her sey*): Almost everything in this store is on sale today.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] 'There is / There are' kalibini tekil ve cogul isimlerle dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Yer bildiren edatlari (in, on, near) dogru sectim mi?

- [ ] Sokak ve yer adlarini buyuk harfle yazdim mi?

- [ ] Istenen kelime sayisina yakin bir paragraf olusturdum mu?


---


## Passage 5: University of London

- **ID:** `raw1_005` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Education & Landmarks | **Sayfa:** 3
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_005_university_of_london.mp3` (*US · en-US-JennyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. The University of London was founded in ____. *(İpucu: year)*

2. The ____, which is near the university, is a famous building in Bloomsbury. *(İpucu: landmark name)*

3. The restaurants in Bloomsbury are not always very ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where is the University of London located?

   - A) In Bloomsbury

   - B) Inside the British Museum

   - C) Outside London

   - D) Near a train station

2. What can you buy at the restaurants and coffee shops in Bloomsbury?

   - A) Things to eat

   - B) University textbooks

   - C) Museum tickets

   - D) Souvenirs and maps


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The British Museum is far away from the University of London.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The University of London is the oldest university in England.


### 2. Reading

The University of London is in Bloomsbury. It was founded in 1836. There are many other famous buildings in Bloomsbury. The British Museum, which is near the university, is one of these buildings. There are also many restaurants and coffee shops, where you can buy things to eat. The restaurants are not always very cheap, but the coffee shops are excellent for the price.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Where is the University of London?

2. Which building is near the university?

3. Where can you eat in Bloomsbury?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* When was the University of London founded?

   - A) In 1836

   - B) In 1863

   - C) In 1936

   - D) In 1830

2. *[Detail]* What does the passage say about the coffee shops in Bloomsbury?

   - A) They offer good value for their price.

   - B) They are much more expensive than the restaurants.

   - C) They are located inside the British Museum.

   - D) They do not serve anything to eat.

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In the passage, the word 'famous' is closest in meaning to ____.

   - A) well-known

   - B) expensive

   - C) modern

   - D) quiet


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summarizing Bloomsbury** (50-70 words): Write a short paragraph summarizing what the passage says about Bloomsbury and the University of London.

- **Task 2 — A Famous Area in My City** (60-90 words): Write a short paragraph about a famous area or building in your city and the places to eat nearby.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **be founded** (*kurulmak, temeli atılmak*): Istanbul University was founded hundreds of years ago.

- **famous** (*ünlü, tanınmış*): Topkapi Palace is a famous building in Istanbul.

- **near** (*yakınında*): The train station is near our school.

- **cheap** (*ucuz*): The food at this small cafe is very cheap.

- **excellent for the price** (*fiyatına göre mükemmel*): The soup here is excellent for the price.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Paragrafı açık ve basit cümlelerle yazdım.

- [ ] Metindeki temel bilgileri (konum, kuruluş yılı, binalar, kafeler) doğru aktardım.

- [ ] Büyük harf ve noktalama işaretlerini kontrol ettim.

- [ ] Geçiş ifadelerini ve basit zaman kiplerini doğru kullandım.

## Passage 6: Jim

- **ID:** `raw1_006` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Work & Education | **Sayfa:** 4
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_006_jim.mp3` (*AU · en-AU-NatashaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Video Sound has ____ offices all over the world. *(İpucu: number)*

2. Mr. Chapman is the first ____ to work at the company's Paris office. *(İpucu: nationality)*

3. Mary goes to the Faculty of ____ at the University of London. *(İpucu: noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is Jim Chapman famous as?

   - A) A famous cameraman

   - B) A university teacher

   - C) An engineer in London

   - D) A musician in Paris

2. Who does Mary live with in London?

   - A) Her friend Paul

   - B) Her brother Jim

   - C) Her sister

   - D) A colleague from Paris


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Jim Chapman is married.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Paul is a student at the University of London.


### 2. Reading

Jim Chapman, a famous cameraman, is the new assistant director of Video Sound, a large film company. It has fifty-two offices all over the world. Mr. Chapman is the first American to work at the company's Paris office in its thirty-year history. Mr. Chapman is not married. He's got a sister - Mary. She lives with her friend Paul in London. She goes to the Faculty of Arts at the University of London. There are also the Faculties of Science, Engineering, Medicine and Music there.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. What is Video Sound?

2. In which city does Mr. Chapman work?

3. Who does Mary live with?

4. What does Mary do?

5. How many faculties are there in the university?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* What is Jim Chapman's new position at Video Sound?

   - A) Assistant director

   - B) Company president

   - C) Chief engineer

   - D) Office manager

2. *[Detail]* What is unusual about Jim Chapman working at the Paris office?

   - A) He is the first American to work there in thirty years.

   - B) He opened the Paris office fifty-two years ago.

   - C) He works there together with his sister Mary.

   - D) He is the only cameraman employed by Video Sound.

3. *[Detail]* How many faculties at the University of London are mentioned in the passage?

   - A) Five

   - B) Four

   - C) Three

   - D) Six


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Introducing Jim and Mary** (50-70 words): Write a short paragraph introducing Jim Chapman, his job, and his sister Mary based on the passage.

- **Task 2 — Someone I Know** (65-90 words): Write a short paragraph about a family member or friend, their job or studies, and where they live.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **assistant director** (*yönetmen yardımcısı*): She works as an assistant director at a television studio.

- **all over the world** (*dünyanın her yerinde*): This international company has offices all over the world.

- **not married** (*evli değil, bekar*): My brother is not married; he lives alone.

- **faculty** (*fakülte*): Our university has a very large Faculty of Engineering.

- **thirty-year history** (*otuz yıllık tarih/geçmiş*): The museum has a rich thirty-year history.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Jim ve Mary hakkındaki temel bilgileri eksiksiz verdim.

- [ ] Geniş zaman (Simple Present) fiil çekimlerini (lives, works, goes) doğru kullandım.

- [ ] Şehir, şirket ve fakülte isimlerini büyük harfle yazdım.

- [ ] Kelime sayısı hedefine uydum.

## Passage 7: Kate

- **ID:** `raw1_007` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Family & Jobs | **Sayfa:** 5, 6
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_007_kate.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Kate works for a young ____ dentist. *(İpucu: nationality)*

2. Bianca teaches in a big school in the ____ of London. *(İpucu: direction)*

3. Kate's brother George doesn't have a job; he is ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where does Catherine live?

   - A) Near Leeds

   - B) In Enfield

   - C) In the north of London

   - D) In Australia

2. How many children does Bianca have?

   - A) Two

   - B) One

   - C) Three

   - D) None


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Catherine thinks the dentist she works for is very nice.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — George is looking for a teaching job in London.


### 2. Reading

My name is Catherine, but I'm called 'Kate' by my friends. I live near Leeds, in the north-east of England. I'm a dental nurse. I work for a young Australian dentist. He's very nice and he's a very good dentist, but I don't like my job very much.

My sister is called Bianca. She's married with two children, a girl and a boy, and lives in a house near London, in a town called Enfield. She's a teacher. She teaches in a big school in the north of London. She likes her job very much.

My brother's name is George. He doesn't have a job. He's unemployed.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. What is Catherine called by her friends?

2. Where does she live?

3. Does she live with her sister?

4. What does she do?

5. Does she like her job?

6. Where is the dentist from?

7. Does Catherine like him?

8. What is her sister's name?

9. Is she married?

10. What does she do?

11. Where does she work?

12. Does she like her job?

13. Does she live in London?

14. Does she live in a flat or a house?

15. What does Catherine's brother do?

16. What's his name?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Which of the following is true about Kate's family?

   - A) Bianca has a daughter and a son.

   - B) George teaches at a school in London.

   - C) Kate works as a dentist near Enfield.

   - D) Bianca is unmarried and lives with Kate.

2. *[Detail]* How do Kate and Bianca feel differently about their jobs?

   - A) Bianca likes her job very much, while Kate does not like hers very much.

   - B) Kate enjoys her job, while Bianca wants to change hers.

   - C) Both sisters dislike working in big cities.

   - D) Neither sister has a job at the moment.

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In the passage, the word 'unemployed' means ____.

   - A) without a job

   - B) working part-time

   - C) living in another country

   - D) studying at a school


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Kate and Her Family** (55-75 words): Write a short summary about Catherine (Kate) and her brother and sister.

- **Task 2 — My Family and Their Jobs** (70-100 words): Write a short paragraph about your family members, where they live, and what they do.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **dental nurse** (*diş hekimi yardımcısı / hemşiresi*): She works as a dental nurse in a busy clinic.

- **north-east** (*kuzeydoğu*): My uncle lives in the north-east of Turkey.

- **married with two children** (*evli ve iki çocuklu*): My sister is married with two children.

- **unemployed** (*işsiz*): Many people in the town are unemployed right now.

- **very much** (*pek / çok fazla (olumsuz cümlede)*): He does not like his job very much.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Kate, Bianca ve George hakkındaki tüm temel bilgileri doğru özetledim.

- [ ] Meslek ve yer ifadelerinde edatları (in, near, for) doğru kullandım.

- [ ] Olumlu ve olumsuz cümle yapılarını (likes / doesn't like) doğru kurdum.

- [ ] Yazım ve noktalama hatalarını gözden geçirdim.

## Passage 8: The Statue of Liberty & The Old England Restaurant

- **ID:** `raw1_008` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Tourism & Dining | **Sayfa:** 7
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_008_the_statue_of_liberty_the_old_en.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. The Statue of Liberty was a present from the ____ to the Americans. *(İpucu: nationality)*

2. The Old England Restaurant serves international food from China, India, and ____. *(İpucu: country)*

3. At the restaurant's nightclub, you can listen to ____ and dance from 11 p.m. to 3 a.m. *(İpucu: noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where do the ferryboats to Liberty Island leave from?

   - A) Battery Park

   - B) Nailsea

   - C) Bristol

   - D) Old England Garden

2. When is the Old England Restaurant open for dinner?

   - A) From 6:30 p.m. until midnight

   - B) From 12:00 to 2:00 p.m.

   - C) From 9:30 a.m. to 3:30 p.m.

   - D) From 11:00 p.m. to 3:00 a.m.


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The last boat back from Liberty Island returns at 5:00 p.m.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The Old England Restaurant is closed on Sundays.


### 2. Reading

The Statue of Liberty

Many tourists visit this famous statue every year. It was a present from the French to the Americans. The statue is on Liberty Island. There are ferryboats from Battery Park to Liberty Island. The trip to the Statue of Liberty takes 15 minutes. Tickets for the boat trip are $6 for adults, $3 for children 3-7 years old, and free for children under 3 years old.

Boats leave Battery Park seven days a week, every half hour from 9:30 am to 3:30 pm. The statue is open until 5 pm. The last boat returns at 5:00 pm.

The Old England Restaurant
Nailsea, near Bristol (Tel: 148952)

Come to the Old England Restaurant in Nailsea for lunch and dinner! Enjoy our excellent international food from China, India, and Italy. We have wonderful steaks, fish, and chicken. Sit in our lovely garden and try our Italian spaghetti and tomato soup. Our restaurant is clean. Our meals are fantastic. They are never cold and always delicious.

Come and have a good time with your friends at our nightclub. Listen to music and dance from 11 pm to 3 am. We serve interesting drinks from different countries.

Do you want to book a table? Phone NOW! We are very busy at lunch-times! Remember, the Old England Restaurant is the restaurant for all occasions: birthdays, business lunches, and evening meals, all at reasonable prices.

Open for lunch: 12:00 - 2:00 pm.
Open for dinner: from 6:30 until midnight.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A. Mark the statements as True (T) or False (F).*

1. You can get to the Statue of Liberty by car.

   - True

   - False

2. You can visit the statue every day.

   - True

   - False

3. The Statue of Liberty closes at 3:30 in the afternoon.

   - True

   - False

4. There is a garden at the Old England Restaurant.

   - True

   - False

5. Many people have lunch at the Old England Restaurant.

   - True

   - False


*B. Answer the following questions.*

6. Why are the meals at the Old England Restaurant fantastic?

7. What can you do at the nightclub? (Write two things.)


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* How long does the ferryboat trip to the Statue of Liberty take?

   - A) 15 minutes

   - B) 30 minutes

   - C) 1 hour

   - D) 5 hours

2. *[Detail]* How much is a boat ticket for a two-year-old child?

   - A) It is free.

   - B) $3

   - C) $6

   - D) $15

3. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of the Old England Restaurant text?

   - A) To advertise the restaurant and invite customers to visit

   - B) To complain about lunch-time crowds in Bristol

   - C) To compare French and Italian cooking traditions

   - D) To explain how to travel from Battery Park to Nailsea


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Visitor Information Summary** (60-80 words): Summarize the key visitor information for both the Statue of Liberty and the Old England Restaurant.

- **Task 2 — My Favorite Place to Visit or Eat** (70-100 words): Write a short advertisement or description for a restaurant or tourist place you like, including its location, food or activities, and opening hours.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **tourist** (*turist, gezgin*): The museum is a popular place for tourists.

- **ferryboat** (*feribot, arabalı vapur*): We took a ferryboat across the river.

- **book a table** (*masa ayırtmak / rezervasyon yapmak*): It is a good idea to book a table for Saturday night.

- **for all occasions** (*her türlü özel gün / durum için*): This hotel is suitable for all occasions, from weddings to meetings.

- **until midnight** (*gece yarısına kadar*): The cafe stays open until midnight on weekends.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Hem Özgürlük Heykeli hem de restoran hakkındaki temel bilgileri (saatler, fiyatlar, hizmetler) doğru aktardım.

- [ ] Saat ve fiyat ifadelerini (from ... to ..., until) doğru yazdım.

- [ ] Cümleleri açık ve akıcı bir dille birleştirdim.

- [ ] Büyük harf ve noktalama kurallarına dikkat ettim.

## Passage 9: Tomiko

- **ID:** `raw1_009` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Work & Daily Life | **Sayfa:** 8, 9
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_009_tomiko.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Tomiko works as an ____ for a large company in London. *(İpucu: noun / job)*

2. She calls her parents every ____. *(İpucu: day of the week)*

3. She comes back from lunch by ____. *(İpucu: time)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Why does Tomiko take the bus to work?

   - A) She does not own a car

   - B) Her apartment is next to the office

   - C) She dislikes driving in London traffic

   - D) The bus is cheaper than driving

2. How long is Tomiko's lunch break?

   - A) Ten minutes

   - B) Twenty minutes

   - C) Thirty minutes

   - D) One hour

3. What does Tomiko do to relax after getting home?

   - A) She studies for college

   - B) She visits her parents in Glasgow

   - C) She cooks dinner for her friends

   - D) She watches TV or listens to music


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tomiko graduated from college last year.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tomiko finishes work at five o'clock.


### 2. Reading

Tomiko is an accountant and she works for a large company in London. She's the youngest of three children. Her parents live in Glasgow far from London. This is Tomiko's first job and she calls her parents every Sunday. Tomiko's parents think she's very young and she can't live alone but Tomiko laughs at that idea. She's twenty-three and finished college last year. She tells them she's happy and everything will be fine.

Tomiko has to be at work by eight o'clock. She goes to work by bus, because she doesn't like to drive in the London traffic. After Tomiko leaves her apartment, she hurries to the bus stop because she doesn't want to miss the bus. Tomiko is a good worker. She's very serious. At twelve o'clock she eats a quick lunch. She comes back from lunch by twelve-twenty. She stops work at four o'clock. She's tired by that time and happy to go home and relax. She gets home around five o'clock, changes her clothes and watches TV, or listens to music.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Where do Tomiko's parents live?

2. Is this Tomiko's first job?

3. How often does she phone her parents?

4. Her parents don't want her to live alone. Why not?

5. When does Tomiko have to be at work?

6. How does Tomiko go to work?

7. Why does she take the bus to work?

8. Does Tomiko work hard?

9. What time does she finish work?

10. What does she do when she gets home?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* How does Tomiko feel about living alone in London?

   - A) She feels lonely and wants to move back to Glasgow.

   - B) She feels confident and happy living on her own.

   - C) She is unhappy with her first job in London.

   - D) She agrees with her parents that she is too young.

2. *[Detail]* About how long does it take Tomiko to travel home from work?

   - A) About twenty minutes

   - B) About thirty minutes

   - C) About one hour

   - D) About two hours

3. *[Main Idea]* What is the passage mainly about?

   - A) Tomiko's new job and daily routine in London

   - B) Why Tomiko's parents want to move to London

   - C) How to travel from Glasgow to London by bus

   - D) Tomiko's college classes and favorite music


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary of Tomiko's Routine** (50-70 words): Write a short summary of Tomiko's job and daily routine in London using your own words.

- **Task 2 — My Daily Routine** (60-80 words): Write a short paragraph about your own daily routine. Explain how you travel to school or work and what you do to relax in the evening.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **accountant** (*muhasebeci*): My sister is an accountant at a bank.

- **hurry** (*acele etmek*): I always hurry to the station in the morning.

- **traffic** (*trafik*): There is a lot of traffic in big cities.

- **quick lunch** (*hizli bir ogle yemegi*): We eat a quick lunch at noon.

- **relax** (*dinlenmek, rahatlamak*): I like to relax and listen to music after work.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Genis zamani (Present Simple) tekil ucuncu sahis (-s) ekleriyle dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Saatleri ve gunluk rutin ifadelerini (by eight o'clock, every Sunday vb.) dogru yazdim mi?

- [ ] Cumlelerimi buyuk harfle baslatip nokta ile bitirdim mi?

- [ ] Parcadan en az uc yeni kelime veya kalip kullandim mi?

## Passage 10: Tomiko, King and Ted

- **ID:** `raw1_010` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** City Life & Pets | **Sayfa:** 9, 10
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_010_tomiko_king_and_ted.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Tomiko has ____ locks on her apartment door. *(İpucu: number)*

2. King protects Tomiko's ____ when she is at work. *(İpucu: noun / place)*

3. Ted works in the same company and is also an ____. *(İpucu: noun / job)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is true about Tomiko and her parents?

   - A) She hides her fear at night from her parents

   - B) She calls her parents whenever she feels afraid

   - C) Her parents do not know she has a dog

   - D) She asks her parents to visit her every night

2. Who does King bark at?

   - A) Only at night

   - B) Only when Ted visits

   - C) At strangers he does not know

   - D) At all of Tomiko's friends

3. What problem does Ted have when visiting Tomiko?

   - A) He lives too far from her apartment

   - B) He is afraid of King and thinks King may bite him

   - C) He does not like working at the same company

   - D) He is angry about the three locks on her door


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tomiko enjoys living in London despite sometimes feeling afraid.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tomiko has a large group of friends in London.


### 2. Reading

Tomiko likes London. There are many places to go and a lot of things to do, but it is not easy to live in a big city far from home. Tomiko never tells her parents, but sometimes she feels afraid at night. Tomiko has three locks on her apartment door and she owns a big dog.

The dog's name is King. He's a good friend and he protects Tomiko. He also protects Tomiko's apartment when she's at work. King is very friendly, but he barks at people he doesn't know. Most people are afraid of King when they hear and see him.

Tomiko doesn't have many friends in London, but she has a boyfriend named Ted. He works in the same company and he is also an accountant. He likes her a lot, but there's one problem. He's afraid of dogs. Last night he came to visit Tomiko. She said to him, "Don't be afraid of King. He won't bite." But Ted doesn't trust King. He's very big and barks too much. Ted's afraid that King may bite him someday.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Why does Tomiko like London?

2. Does she tell her parents about all her problems?

3. When does she feel afraid?

4. Why is it difficult to get into Tomiko's apartment when she's not there?

5. Who does King protect? What does he protect?

6. Who does King bark at?

7. How do people feel when they hear and see King?

8. Does Tomiko have a lot of friends in London?

9. Where does her boyfriend work?

10. Why is Ted afraid to go to Tomiko's apartment?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* Why does Tomiko probably hide her fear from her parents?

   - A) Because her parents do not like London

   - B) Because her parents are afraid of King

   - C) Because she does not want them to worry about her living alone

   - D) Because she rarely talks to her parents

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does "trust King" mean in the sentence "But Ted doesn't trust King"?

   - A) Believe that King is safe and will not bite him

   - B) Remember King's name when he visits

   - C) Play with King outside the apartment

   - D) Take care of King while Tomiko is at work

3. *[Detail]* What do we know about Ted's job?

   - A) He is her neighbor in the same apartment building.

   - B) He is King's former owner.

   - C) He works at a different company in Glasgow.

   - D) He is an accountant at Tomiko's company.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Tomiko, King, and Ted Summary** (50-70 words): Summarize why Tomiko has King and what problem Ted faces when visiting her.

- **Task 2 — Living in a Big City** (60-85 words): Do you think it is easy or difficult to live in a big city far from home? Write a short paragraph explaining your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **feel afraid** (*korkmus hissetmek*): She locks the door at night because she feels afraid.

- **protect** (*korumak*): My dog protects our house when we are away.

- **bark at** (*havlamak*): The dog barks at people it does not know.

- **trust** (*guvenmek*): I trust my best friend completely.

- **bite** (*isirmak*): Don't worry, that small dog won't bite.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Duygulari ve nedenleri (afraid of, because vb.) dogru ifade ettim mi?

- [ ] Olumsuz cumlelerde 'doesn't' ve 'never' kullanimina dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Olaylari mantikli bir sira ile (Tomiko -> King -> Ted) anlattim mi?

- [ ] Kelime sayisi hedefime uydum mu?

## Passage 11: The Garston Family

- **ID:** `raw1_011` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Family & Pets | **Sayfa:** 10
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_011_the_garston_family.mp3` (*US · en-US-JennyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Mr. and Mrs. Garston live in a small ____ near London. *(İpucu: noun / place)*

2. The dogs eat ____, biscuits, and special dog food. *(İpucu: noun / food)*

3. Mr. Garston comes home at ____. *(İpucu: number / time)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Why does Mr. Garston buy dog food in London?

   - A) The food in the village is too expensive

   - B) The village shops do not sell dog food

   - C) He only visits London on weekends

   - D) His dogs only like biscuits from London

2. Who does not like Bruce and Spot?

   - A) Mrs. Garston and the postman

   - B) The London shopkeepers

   - C) The neighbours and the postman

   - D) Only the neighbours' cat

3. Why are the dogs happy when Mr. Garston gets home?

   - A) They go for a long evening walk with him

   - B) He brings them new toys from London

   - C) They get to play with the neighbours' cat

   - D) He feeds them biscuits in the morning


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The Garston family lives in the center of London.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Mrs. Garston also works in London every day.


### 2. Reading

Mr. and Mrs. Garston live in a small village near London. They've got two dogs. Their names are Bruce and Spot. The dogs eat meat, biscuits and special dog food. Mr. Garston buys their food in London because the shops in their village don't sell dog food. The Garstons have got neighbours, too, but they don't like Bruce and Spot because they've got a cat. The postman doesn't like the dogs, either.

Mr. Garston goes to London every day because he works there. He comes home at six. The dogs always wait for him. They stand near the door and bark. They are happy because they go for a long walk in the evening with Mr. Garston.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Do Mr. and Mrs. Garston live in London?

2. What do the dogs eat?

3. Why does Mr. Garston buy dog food in London?

4. Why don't the neighbours like the dogs?

5. When does Mr. Garston come home every day?

6. Why do the dogs always wait for Mr. Garston?


*Reference*

7. What does 'they' refer to in "The Garstons have got neighbours, too, but they don't like Bruce and Spot"?

8. What does 'there' refer to in "Mr. Garston goes to London every day because he works there."?

9. What does 'They' refer to in "They stand near the door and bark. They are happy because they go for a long walk in the evening with Mr. Garston."?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* Why is it convenient for Mr. Garston to buy dog food in London?

   - A) London is closer to their house than the village shops.

   - B) Mrs. Garston asks him to walk the dogs in London.

   - C) The postman refuses to deliver food to the village.

   - D) He works in London and the village shops do not sell it.

2. *[Detail]* What does the passage tell us about the neighbours and the postman?

   - A) Bruce and Spot live in London with the postman.

   - B) Both the neighbours and the postman have a negative feeling toward the dogs.

   - C) The postman loves dogs, especially Bruce and Spot.

   - D) The neighbours have two dogs and a cat.

3. *[Main Idea]* What is the main topic of the passage?

   - A) The Garston family and their two dogs

   - B) How to train dogs to walk with a postman

   - C) Shops and businesses in a small village

   - D) Why cats and dogs cannot live in London


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary of The Garston Family** (50-70 words): Write a short summary of the Garston family, their dogs, and why their neighbours dislike the dogs.

- **Task 2 — Having Pets at Home** (60-80 words): Do you like having pets at home? Write a short paragraph giving reasons for your answer.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **village** (*koy*): My grandparents live in a quiet village.

- **neighbours** (*komsular*): Our neighbours are very friendly and helpful.

- **postman** (*postaci*): The postman brings letters every morning.

- **wait for** (*beklemek*): We always wait for the school bus at the corner.

- **go for a long walk** (*uzun bir yuruyuse cikmak*): They go for a long walk in the park on Sundays.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Zamirleri (they, there, them) net ve dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Nedensellik bildiren 'because' baglacini dogru yerlestirdim mi?

- [ ] Sahiplik ifadelerini (have got / 've got) dogru yazdim mi?

- [ ] Paragrafi mantikli ve anlasilir sekilde tamamladim mi?

Cinema 1
Another provocative and striking film by the most applauded film maker in the world! Claude Lelouch.
LONG LIVE LIFE
Minimum age: 14 years
2.00  4.00  6.00  8.00  10.00
Starring Charlotte Rampling, Michel Piccoli

A Bertrand Blier film:
SEPARATE ROOMS

Today at Cinema Akun
Sissy Spacek in MARIE

3.45  6.30  9.15

   - C) Marie

   - D) The Color Purple

2. *[Detail]* Which film is advertised as being based on real events?

   - A) Long Live Life

   - B) Separate Rooms

   - C) The Color Purple

   - D) Marie

- **Task 2 — Choosing a Film** (65-90 words): Which of the four films would you choose to watch? Write a short paragraph explaining your choice and why you want to see it.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **starring** (*basrolunde oynamak*): The movie stars two famous actors from France.

- **directed by** (*tarafindan yonetilmek*): The film is directed by a young Italian filmmaker.

- **true story** (*gercek bir hikaye*): This film is based on a true story about a brave nurse.

- **minimum age** (*asgari yas siniri*): There is a minimum age of 14 for this action movie.

- **moving** (*duygulandirici, etkileyici*): It was a moving story that made everyone cry.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Yazimda neden-sonuc iliskisini acikca belirttim mi?

## Passage 13: Plaza Shopping Centre: A Shopping Experience Without Equal

- **ID:** `raw1_013` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Shopping & Transportation | **Sayfa:** 12
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_013_plaza_shopping_centre_a_shopping.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Plaza Shopping is located in the centre of ____, next to the South Coach Station. *(İpucu: town name)*

2. The car park has space for ____ cars across three floors. *(İpucu: number)*

3. Opening hours for the centre are 10 a.m. to 10 p.m., Monday to ____. *(İpucu: day of the week)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Which form of transport is NOT mentioned for reaching Plaza Shopping?

   - A) By bus

   - B) By train

   - C) By boat

   - D) By car

2. What feature makes entering and leaving the car park easier?

   - A) A free shuttle bus service

   - B) Two independent ramps for entrances and exits

   - C) Automated ticket machines

   - D) A direct underground tunnel

3. What is open on Sundays at Plaza Shopping?

   - A) Only the bus station next door

   - B) All of the clothes shops

   - C) Only the entrance ramps

   - D) The Picnic department and the car park


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Visitors must pay a small fee to use the three-floor car park.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The advertisement suggests that shoppers from the area do not need to travel to London anymore.


### 2. Reading

To begin with, think of the location. What other shopping centre can equal it? Plaza Shopping is in the centre of Harlow, next to the South Coach Station.

Easy to reach by bus, train or car. It's on your daily route. Free parking. 3 floors with 35,000 square metres of space. Space for 12,000 cars. To make access even easier, there are two independent ramps for entrances and exits.

Plaza Shopping exists to make life easier. There is no need to go into London for shopping any longer. Opening hours are 10 am to 10 pm, Monday to Saturday. The Picnic department is open on Sundays, too, and so is the car park. Come to Plaza Shopping. You will find everything you want. Come and see!


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Mark the statements as True (T) or False (F).*

1. The Plaza Shopping Centre is in London.

   - True

   - False

2. You can go to the Plaza Shopping Centre by car only.

   - True

   - False

3. The Plaza Shopping Centre has a big car park.

   - True

   - False

4. You can shop at the Plaza Shopping Centre 24 hours a day.

   - True

   - False

5. The Picnic department is open seven days a week.

   - True

   - False


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of this passage?

   - A) To persuade local shoppers to visit Plaza Shopping Centre instead of going to London

   - B) To complain about heavy traffic near the South Coach Station in Harlow

   - C) To announce that the Picnic department will soon close on Sundays

   - D) To compare bus and train ticket prices between Harlow and London

2. *[Inference]* What can be inferred from the statement 'There is no need to go into London for shopping any longer'?

   - A) London is much closer to the South Coach Station than Harlow is

   - B) Shops in London offer free parking for 12,000 cars

   - C) People in Harlow previously had to travel to London for major shopping trips

   - D) Plaza Shopping is only open to commuters travelling by train

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does the word 'independent' most closely mean in 'there are two independent ramps for entrances and exits'?

   - A) Expensive to use

   - B) Separate from each other

   - C) Recently repaired

   - D) Underground


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Shopping Centre Summary** (60-80 words): Write a short summary describing the location, parking facilities, and opening hours of Plaza Shopping Centre based on the passage.

- **Task 2 — Large Shopping Centres vs. Small Shops** (80-110 words): Do you prefer shopping at a large shopping centre or in small local shops? Give reasons for your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **location** (*konum, yer*): The new library is in a quiet location near the park.

- **reach** (*ulaşmak*): It is easy to reach the airport by train.

- **route** (*güzergâh, rota*): I always take the same route to work every morning.

- **opening hours** (*açılış saatleri*): The museum's opening hours are 9 am to 5 pm.

- **ramp** (*rampa, eğimli yol*): The wide ramp allows wheelchairs to enter the building easily.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Alışveriş merkezinin konumunu, ulaşım seçeneklerini ve çalışma saatlerini doğru aktardım.

- [ ] Otopark kapasitesi ve Pazar günü açık olan bölümler gibi temel ayrıntılara yer verdim.

- [ ] Fikir yazısında görüşümü destekleyen en az iki somut neden sundum.

- [ ] Zaman ifadelerini (from ... to ..., Monday to Saturday, on Sundays) doğru kullandım.

## Passage 14: The International Hotel

- **ID:** `raw1_014` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Hotel Services & Facilities | **Sayfa:** 13
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_014_the_international_hotel.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Hotel residents can use the ____ free of charge. *(İpucu: hotel facility)*

2. The Coffee Shop serves snacks or meals from 8 a.m. to ____ every day. *(İpucu: time of day)*

3. Both the Flower Shop and the Exchange Bureau are closed on ____. *(İpucu: day of the week)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. When does The International Restaurant serve dinner?

   - A) 7:00 a.m. to 11:00 a.m.

   - B) 12:30 p.m. to 2:30 p.m.

   - C) 6:30 p.m. to 10:30 p.m.

   - D) 11:00 a.m. to 1:00 a.m.

2. Which floor has the Gift Shop and the Boutique?

   - A) The First Floor

   - B) The Ground Floor

   - C) The Basement

   - D) The Second Floor

3. Where in the hotel can guests change foreign currency?

   - A) At the Reception Desk

   - B) At the Gift Shop

   - C) At the Coffee Shop

   - D) At the Exchange Bureau


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — If a guest wants a snack at 11:15 p.m., the Coffee Shop is still open.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Max's Hairdressing and Beauty Salon is open to women only.


### 2. Reading

Floor Directory:
Second Floor
First Floor: Gift Shop, Boutique
Ground Floor: Flower Shop, Swimming Pool, Reception Desk, Main Entrance
Basement: Car Park, Men's Changing, Women's Changing, Kitchen

THE INTERNATIONAL HOTEL
At your service...

Boutique. High quality clothes for men and women.

Car Park. Park your car in safety, in the hotel car park.

Coffee Shop. Snacks or meals from 8 am to midnight every day.

Flower Shop. Flowers delivered. Open 9 am - 5.30 pm. Closed on Sunday.

Gift Shop. Gift items, newspapers, magazines, books, pens, stamps.

Sauna. Free to residents.

Swimming Pool. Open 7 am - 9 pm.

Exchange Bureau. You can change your foreign currency and traveller's cheques in this hotel. Closed on Sunday.

The International Bar. Open 11 am - 1 am.

The International Restaurant. Open 7 - 11 am for breakfast, 12.30 pm - 2.30 pm for lunch, 6.30 pm - 10.30 pm for dinner.

Max's Hairdressing and Beauty Salon. Have your hair done by Max. Open to men and women.

We hope you enjoy your stay.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Answer the following questions.*

1. How many days a week is the Exchange Bureau open?

2. What is there opposite the Coffee Shop?

3. Where does Max work in the hotel?

4. What time does the dinner service start?

5. You want to buy a stamp. Which floor do you go to?

6. It is 3 pm and you are very hungry. Where can you eat in the hotel?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Which hotel service stays open for the longest total time each day?

   - A) The Swimming Pool

   - B) The Coffee Shop

   - C) The International Bar

   - D) The Flower Shop

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In 'Sauna. Free to residents', who are the 'residents'?

   - A) People who live in the city nearby

   - B) Hotel staff working in the kitchen

   - C) Guests who are staying at the hotel

   - D) Customers visiting only the Gift Shop

3. *[Inference]* Which services are open if a guest wakes up at 7:15 am?

   - A) Only the Coffee Shop

   - B) Only the Flower Shop

   - C) The International Bar and the Boutique

   - D) The Swimming Pool and The International Restaurant


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Hotel Facilities Guide** (65-85 words): Write a brief guide summarizing the main facilities and dining options available at The International Hotel.

- **Task 2 — Important Hotel Facilities** (80-110 words): Which hotel facilities are most important to you when you travel, and why?


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **foreign currency** (*yabancı para birimi, döviz*): Travelers can change their foreign currency at the airport.

- **in safety** (*güvenli bir şekilde*): Always park your car in safety inside a locked garage.

- **residents** (*otelde kalan misafirler, sakinler*): The hotel offers free Wi-Fi to all residents.

- **breakfast** (*kahvaltı*): The hotel restaurant serves breakfast from 7 am to 10 am.

- **gift shop** (*hediyelik eşya dükkanı*): I usually buy a newspaper and some stamps at the gift shop.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Otelin yemek, dinlenme ve alışveriş olanaklarını doğru saatlerle özetledim.

- [ ] Pazar günleri kapalı olan hizmetleri açıkça belirttim.

- [ ] Görüş yazımda bir otel seçerken benim için en önemli özellikleri örneklerle açıkladım.

- [ ] Saat ve gün edatlarını (from ... to ..., on Sunday, at midnight) hatasız kullandım.

## Passage 15: Job Advertisements

- **ID:** `raw1_015` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Job Advertisements & Careers | **Sayfa:** 15
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_015_job_advertisements.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. A reporter for The Selton Times must have an interest in sport or ____. *(İpucu: noun)*

2. The school bus driver must be good with ____ and do some cleaning. *(İpucu: plural noun)*

3. For the Electronic Engineer position at P.K. Electronics, the salary is ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What age group is wanted for the reporter job at The Selton Times?

   - A) Ages 16 to 19

   - B) Ages 30 to 50

   - C) Ages 18 to 30

   - D) Any age over 50

2. How should a candidate apply for the Office Assistant job?

   - A) Call John Brown on 676-4327

   - B) Send handwritten details to Mr. B.K. Graves

   - C) Visit 41 Stoughton House in Suffolk

   - D) Show micro-computer experience to The Selton Times

3. Which two jobs specifically state that a driving licence is necessary?

   - A) Reporter and Electronic Engineer

   - B) Office Assistant and School Bus Driver

   - C) Reporter and Office Assistant

   - D) Electronic Engineer and Office Assistant


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The school bus driver works seven hours a day, from early morning until early afternoon.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The Electronic Engineer job pays more money than the Reporter job.


### 2. Reading

REPORTER WANTED
Reporter wanted for The Selton Times, aged 18-30. Must have a driving licence. Typing necessary. Interest in sport/theatre. 40 hours/week. Tel: 7564529.
The Selton Times

SCHOOL BUS DRIVER
Driver, aged 30-50, wanted for school bus. Some cleaning also. Must be good with children. 7.00 am - 2.00 pm only. Contact John Brown. Tel: 676-4327.

OFFICE ASSISTANT
A varied and interesting position with a good telephone manner, good typing, nice personality and lots of common sense. If you are 16 to 19 then send handwritten details to:
Mr. B.K. Graves, H.G. White, Whitehall Road, Peterborough.

ELECTRONIC ENGINEER
Required, micro-computer experience, driving licence necessary. Salary negotiable.
P.K. Electronics, 41 Stoughton House, Suffolk.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Mark the statements as True (T) or False (F).*

1. You are 16 and you can type well. You can work for The Selton Times.

   - True

   - False

2. The school bus driver starts work early in the morning and finishes late in the evening.

   - True

   - False

3. The company called P.K. Electronics is in Suffolk.

   - True

   - False

4. Mr. Graves is looking for an office assistant.

   - True

   - False


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Which requirement is listed for the Office Assistant position?

   - A) Micro-computer experience

   - B) A driving licence

   - C) An interest in sport or theatre

   - D) Good typing and a nice personality

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does 'Salary negotiable' mean in the Electronic Engineer advertisement?

   - A) The job does not pay a salary

   - B) The pay can be discussed and agreed upon

   - C) The salary is paid every week in cash

   - D) Only experienced drivers can apply for pay

3. *[Inference]* If a 22-year-old has a driving licence, types well, and loves football, which job matches all their qualifications?

   - A) School Bus Driver

   - B) Office Assistant

   - C) Reporter for The Selton Times

   - D) None of the advertised jobs


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Job Advertisements Summary** (70-90 words): Summarize the four job advertisements by mentioning the job titles, key requirements, and who is eligible to apply.

- **Task 2 — Choosing a Job** (80-110 words): Which of the four advertised jobs would you most like to apply for, and why?


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **driving licence** (*ehliyet, sürücü belgesi*): You need a driving licence before you can work as a delivery driver.

- **telephone manner** (*telefonda konuşma adabı / üslubu*): Workers in a call centre must have a good telephone manner.

- **common sense** (*sağduyu, pratik zeka*): Solving everyday problems quickly requires lots of common sense.

- **handwritten details** (*el yazısıyla yazılmış bilgiler*): She sent handwritten details when applying for the office job.

- **negotiable** (*pazarlığa açık, görüşülebilir*): The company says the starting salary is negotiable depending on experience.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] İlanlardaki yaş aralıklarını, çalışma saatlerini ve gerekli becerileri doğru özetledim.

- [ ] Başvuru yapılacak kişileri ve şirket isimlerini karıştırmadan yazdım.

- [ ] Fikir paragrafımda seçtiğim mesleği neden tercih ettiğimi kişisel becerilerimle ilişkilendirdim.

- [ ] Meslekler ve iş ilanlarıyla ilgili temel kalıpları (driving licence, common sense, salary negotiable) doğru kullandım.

## Passage 16: Young Love at The Cave

- **ID:** `raw1_016` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Music & Daily Jobs | **Sayfa:** 16
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_016_young_love_at_the_cave.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Bette Lord works in Manchester, where she teaches ____. *(İpucu: noun)*

2. At the weekend, the band sings in pubs and ____. *(İpucu: plural noun)*

3. Tickets for tonight's concert at The Cave cost ____. *(İpucu: price)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is Sarah Jones's daytime job?

   - A) She drives a taxi

   - B) She is a secretary

   - C) She teaches music

   - D) She works at The Cave

2. What are Sarah's usual office working hours?

   - A) From 8:30 to 5:00

   - B) From 9:00 to 8:30

   - C) From 9:00 to 5:00

   - D) From Monday to Saturday

3. What time is Young Love playing tonight at The Cave?

   - A) At 8:30

   - B) At 9:00

   - C) At 5:00

   - D) At 4:00


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The members of Young Love have regular daytime jobs in addition to playing in the band.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Sarah Jones is working in her Manchester office today before going to London.


### 2. Reading

Sarah Jones, Pete Parsons and Bette Lord work in Manchester. Sarah is a secretary. Pete drives a taxi and Bette teaches music. But tonight they are in London. They're playing at The Cave. Who are they? They're the new band YOUNG LOVE. From Monday to Friday they work in Manchester. At the weekend they sing in pubs and discos. They've got two jobs and they work very hard.

Sarah Jones says, 'It isn't easy. I'm very busy. My office opens at 9.00 and I usually work from 9.00 to 5.00. I'm not working today because we're singing tonight in London.'

YOUNG LOVE are playing at The Cave, Lower Harrington St. at 8.30. Tickets are £4.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Answer the following questions.*

1. What is YOUNG LOVE?

2. Why are they in London?

3. Do they sing in Manchester?

4. Where does Sarah usually work?

5. Why isn't she working today?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* Why does Sarah Jones say 'It isn't easy'?

   - A) They do not like living in Manchester

   - B) Playing at The Cave is very expensive for them

   - C) Balancing a daytime job and a music band makes her very busy

   - D) Driving a taxi is harder than teaching music

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does 'playing' mean in 'They're playing at The Cave'?

   - A) Performing music

   - B) Playing a sport

   - C) Working in an office

   - D) Buying tickets

3. *[Detail]* Who are the members of Young Love?

   - A) Four office workers in London

   - B) Two taxi drivers from Manchester

   - C) Three music teachers at a school

   - D) Three people with weekday jobs who also perform in a band


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — About the Band Young Love** (55-75 words): Write a short summary about the band Young Love, their weekday jobs, and tonight's concert.

- **Task 2 — Having Two Jobs** (75-100 words): Would you like to have two jobs like the members of Young Love? Why or why not?


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **drive a taxi** (*taksi şoförlüğü yapmak, taksi sürmek*): My brother drives a taxi in the city centre.

- **at the weekend** (*hafta sonu*): At the weekend, we usually visit our grandparents.

- **work very hard** (*çok çalışmak*): Students must work very hard before important exams.

- **be very busy** (*çok meşgul olmak*): I'm very busy this morning because I have three meetings.

- **tickets are ...** (*biletler ... sterlin/lira tutarındadır*): Concert tickets are £4 at the door.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Üç grup üyesinin hafta içi mesleklerini ve hafta sonu müzik yaptıklarını yazdım.

- [ ] Londra'daki konserin yerini, saatini ve bilet fiyatını doğru verdim.

- [ ] Görüş yazımda iki iş yapmanın olumlu ve zor yönlerinden bahsettim.

- [ ] Geniş zaman (usually work) ve şimdiki zaman (are playing tonight) yapılarını doğru ayırt ettim.

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. West End School of English offers small classes with a maximum of ____ students per class. *(İpucu: number)*

2. Edwards College of English and Further Studies is located in ____, England. *(İpucu: city name)*

3. Susan Treynor describes the Berkeley Quartet as a great ____ group. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where are The Big Boys performing their new song?

   - A) At the Coliseum on St Martin's Lane

   - B) At the Concert Hall in Bristol

   - C) At the University of Texas Social Club

   - D) At West End School of English

2. What courses are offered by Edwards College of English and Further Studies?

   - A) French, German, and Spanish courses

   - B) General English, Specialist English, and Computer Courses

   - C) Cambridge First Certificate and Opera lessons

   - D) Rock music and classical guitar lessons

3. How much is admission to see the Berkeley Quartet?

   - A) 7 dollars 50

   - B) 8 pounds

   - C) 18 pounds 50

   - D) 12 dollars


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — West End School of English provides an accommodation service for its students.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The Foreign Friends Agency only helps people who want to practise French.


### 2. Reading

WEST END SCHOOL OF ENGLISH
21 Old Street, London W2 1QB
Telephone: 01-402 7235
Telex: 860 703 WEND
We offer:
- small classes (maximum 12 students / class)
- friendly atmosphere
- social club
- special classes for Cambridge First Certificate
- accommodation service
- central London location.

EDWARDS COLLEGE OF ENGLISH and Further Studies
Director: Richard James BA (Hons.), MA
* General English
* Specialist English (Business, Import/Export, Hotel Operations)
* Computer Courses
For further information write or telephone to:
Edwards College of English and Further Studies, Edwards House, Bristol, CD 3TP, England.
Tel: (0843) 943276. Telex: 635569

If you are interested in practising your French (or German, or Spanish, or Italian),
WHY NOT GET A PEN-FRIEND?
We have contacts in many countries.
Write to:
FOREIGN FRIENDS AGENCY
23 Ramford Rd., Bristol, BS3 7HZ

The BIG BOYS sing their new song: YOU'RE DRIVING ME CRAZY at the University of Texas Social Club, Fri. and Sat. 8:00 p.m.
Do you like rock music?
Don't miss this concert.
808 University Avenue, 555-6000.

Opera
Coliseum (St Martin's Lane, WC2, 836 3161 cc 240 5258):
English National Opera revives Madam Butterfly, in Graham Vick's subtle production (8.00 p.m. £18.50).

The BERKELEY QUARTET
now playing at the Concert Hall, 8:30 p.m.
"A great classical music group" - Susan Treynor of the Evening Star.
Admission $7.50


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*True / False*

1. You want to study English in London. You phone 01-402 7235 for information.

   - True

   - False

2. A ticket for Madam Butterfly is £8.00.

   - True

   - False

3. The rock concert starts at eight in the evening.

   - True

   - False

4. You can get information about the Edwards College of English from the school secretary, Mr. Richard James.

   - True

   - False

5. The pen-friend club is in Brighton.

   - True

   - False


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What do these advertisements mainly promote?

   - A) University textbooks and exam schedules

   - B) News reports from London and Bristol

   - C) Language courses, a pen-friend service, and musical events

   - D) Travel agencies and hotel bookings

2. *[Detail]* Which two advertisers are based in the same city?

   - A) West End School of English and the Opera Coliseum

   - B) Edwards College of English and the Foreign Friends Agency

   - C) The Big Boys concert and the Berkeley Quartet

   - D) West End School of English and the Foreign Friends Agency

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In the opera advertisement, what does the word "revives" most likely mean?

   - A) Cancels

   - B) Writes

   - C) Translates

   - D) Stages again


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Comparing Two Language Schools** (60-80 words): Write a short summary comparing the two English schools in the advertisements. Mention their locations and what they offer.

- **Task 2 — Choosing an Event or Service** (80-110 words): Which event or service from the advertisements would you choose (the rock concert, the opera, the classical concert, or a pen-friend), and why? Give at least two reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **accommodation service** (*konaklama hizmeti*): Our hotel has an accommodation service for guests who stay for a month.

- **friendly atmosphere** (*samimi / sicak ortam*): This language school is popular because it has a very friendly atmosphere.

- **further information** (*daha fazla bilgi*): For further information about the course, please call our office.

- **admission** (*giris (ucreti)*): Admission to the museum is free for students on Sundays.

- **pen-friend** (*mektup arkadasi*): I want to get a pen-friend from Spain to practise my Spanish.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Reklamlardaki yer, saat ve ucret bilgilerini dogru aktardim mi?

- [ ] Cumlelerimde genis zaman ve uygun edatlari (at, in, on) dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Secimimin nedenini en az iki somut ayrintiyla acikladim mi?

- [ ] Yazimi imla ve noktalama hatalarina karsi kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 18: San Francisco

- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_018_san_francisco.mp3` (*AU · en-AU-NatashaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. San Francisco is located near the Pacific Ocean in the state of ____. *(İpucu: state name)*

2. The Golden Gate Bridge is described as the ____ of San Francisco. *(İpucu: noun)*

3. Alcatraz used to be a famous ____, though today only tourists visit it. *(İpucu: noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Who were the first people mentioned to live in San Francisco?

   - A) People from China

   - B) People from Spain

   - C) People from Italy

   - D) People from Austria

2. What can visitors watch while eating seafood at Fisherman's Wharf?

   - A) Traditional Chinese houses and post boxes

   - B) Prisoners walking around Alcatraz Island

   - C) Ships passing under and cars crossing the Golden Gate Bridge

   - D) Nightclubs, cinemas, and theatres in the city centre

3. Why do many young people want to live in San Francisco?

   - A) Because it is the capital of California

   - B) Because it has the biggest Chinese population in America

   - C) Because you can take a boat trip every morning

   - D) Because of its sunny weather and lively nightlife


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — San Francisco is the capital city of California.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Chinatown looks very similar to China because of its streets, houses, and post boxes.


### 2. Reading

San Francisco is in the State of California in the west of the United States. It is near the Pacific Ocean. People think it is the capital of California, but it isn't. First, people from Spain lived there. Today, Austrians, Italians, Chinese and others live in the city with Americans.

The Chinese live in Chinatown. There are many Chinese restaurants there. The houses, post boxes and telephones in the streets are very interesting. This part of the town is exactly like China.

Fisherman's Wharf is another place where tourists want to go. You can find a table in a nice little restaurant near the sea there. You can eat seafood and look at the Golden Gate Bridge. You can watch the ships passing under it and the cars crossing it. The Golden Gate Bridge is the symbol of San Francisco.

After you finish your meal, you can take a boat trip to Alcatraz. Alcatraz was a famous prison between 1866 and 1920, but only tourists go there now.

San Francisco is different from other American cities. It is more European. The sun always shines there and there are a lot of things you can do at night - you can go to the nightclubs, discos, restaurants, cinemas and theatres. That's why a lot of young people want to live in San Francisco.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Where is San Francisco?

2. Is it the capital of California?

3. Who came to San Francisco first?

4. Which interesting places do tourists visit in San Francisco? (3 places)

5. Who lives in Chinatown?

6. What can you watch in a restaurant at Fisherman's Wharf?

7. What is the symbol of San Francisco?

8. What was Alcatraz?

9. Are there any prisoners in Alcatraz today?

10. Why is San Francisco different from other American cities?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of this passage?

   - A) To introduce San Francisco's culture, attractions, and atmosphere

   - B) To explain the history of California's state government

   - C) To compare European restaurants with American restaurants

   - D) To tell the story of famous prisoners in Alcatraz

2. *[Inference]* What can be inferred about San Francisco from the passage?

   - A) San Francisco has very cold and rainy weather

   - B) Few tourists visit Fisherman's Wharf during the day

   - C) San Francisco is home to people from many different cultures

   - D) Alcatraz is still closed to visitors today

3. *[Reference]* In the sentence "You can watch the ships passing under it and the cars crossing it," what does "it" refer to?

   - A) Fisherman's Wharf

   - B) The Golden Gate Bridge

   - C) A little restaurant

   - D) The Pacific Ocean


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Describing San Francisco** (60-80 words): Summarize the main attractions of San Francisco and explain why young people like living there.

- **Task 2 — A Trip to San Francisco** (80-110 words): Would you like to visit San Francisco? Explain which places in the passage you want to see most and why.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **symbol** (*sembol, simge*): The Eiffel Tower is the most famous symbol of Paris.

- **take a boat trip** (*tekne turu / tekne gezisi*): We took a boat trip across the bay in the afternoon.

- **pass under** (*altindan gecmek*): The tourists watched the large ships passing under the bridge.

- **capital** (*baskent*): Ankara is the capital of Turkey.

- **tourist** (*turist, gezgin*): The museum is open to tourists every day of the week.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Metindeki uc onemli turistik yeri ve ozelliklerini yazdim mi?

- [ ] Sebep-sonuc baglaclarini (because, so, that's why) dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Sehir ve ulus isimlerinin bas harflerini buyuk yazdim mi?

- [ ] Paragrafimin kelime sayisi hedef araliga uygun mu?
