## Passage 19: Letter from Halima and Ahmed

- **ID:** `raw1_019` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Travel & Sightseeing | **Sayfa:** 20, 21
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_019_letter_from_halima_and_ahmed.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. About ____ people visit Washington D.C. every year. *(İpucu: number phrase)*

2. Halima and Ahmed learned that the White House was built in ____. *(İpucu: year)*

3. When they arrived at the White House, they saw two ____ chasing each other in the garden. *(İpucu: plural noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Who are Halima and Ahmed visiting Washington D.C. with?

   - A) With their American friend, Tom

   - B) With their Aunt Samira

   - C) With a group of school students

   - D) Alone without any friends

2. Which places did Halima and Ahmed visit on Friday?

   - A) The White House and the Air and Space Museum

   - B) Only the Lincoln Memorial and their hotel

   - C) The Lincoln Memorial, the Vietnam Veterans Memorial, and the Capitol

   - D) The White House garden and several restaurants

3. Why couldn't Halima and Ahmed enter the National Air and Space Museum on Saturday?

   - A) Because it was closed for repairs all weekend

   - B) Because they lost their tickets at the hotel

   - C) Because they stayed at the White House until midnight

   - D) Because they arrived after it closed at 7 p.m.


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — John Adams was the first president to live in the White House.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Halima and Ahmed plan to stay in Washington D.C. for one month.


### 2. Reading

Washington D.C. is the capital of the United States. Fifteen million people visit the city every year. Halima and Ahmed, her brother, left their home in Egypt and travelled to the United States last week. They are visiting Washington D.C. with their American friend, Tom. Here is the letter they wrote to their aunt.

Dear Aunt Samira,

We arrived in Washington, D.C. three days ago. What a great city! On Friday we took a tour. We saw many historical sites: the Lincoln Memorial, the Vietnam Veterans Memorial, and the Capitol. On Saturday, we were busy, too. We left our hotel and went to the White House. This is where the President lives. It's a big, white building. We learned that it was made in 1792 and John Adams was the first president to live there. When we arrived at the White House, we saw two dogs in the garden. They were chasing each other. We took a picture of them. Later we visited some of the museums. We wanted to see the National Air and Space Museum too, but it closes at 7 p.m. and we went there at 7:30 p.m. We hope to see it tomorrow.

Halima and Ahmed


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. How many people visit Washington D.C. every year?

2. Where are Halima and Ahmed from?

3. What is Ahmed's aunt's name?

4. Do Halima and Ahmed like the city?

5. When do they visit the Capitol?

6. What are the Lincoln Memorial, the Vietnam Memorial, and the Capitol?

7. Are Halima and Ahmed staying at Tom's house?

8. What's the White House like?

9. What do Halima and Ahmed take a picture of?

10. Did they see the National Air and Space Museum on Saturday?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Tone & Attitude]* How do Halima and Ahmed feel in their letter to their aunt?

   - A) Tired and unhappy with their hotel

   - B) Excited and enthusiastic about their trip

   - C) Worried about returning to Egypt soon

   - D) Bored by the historical buildings

2. *[Detail]* Which statement is true according to the letter?

   - A) They arrived in Washington, D.C. on Saturday evening

   - B) They live in Tom's house near the White House

   - C) They visited historical sites on Friday and museums on Saturday

   - D) They met the President inside the White House garden

3. *[Reference]* In the sentence "We hope to see it tomorrow," what does "it" refer to?

   - A) The National Air and Space Museum

   - B) The White House garden

   - C) The Lincoln Memorial

   - D) The Capitol building


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — A Summary of Their Trip** (60-80 words): Write a short summary of what Halima and Ahmed did on Friday and Saturday in Washington D.C.

- **Task 2 — A Holiday Letter Home** (80-110 words): Imagine you are visiting a famous city on holiday. Write a short letter or email to a friend or family member describing what you saw yesterday and what you plan to do tomorrow.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **historical sites** (*tarihi yerler*): Every capital city has many historical sites for tourists to explore.

- **take a tour** (*tura cikmak*): On our first day in London, we took a tour by bus.

- **chase each other** (*birbirini kovalamak*): The two playful dogs were chasing each other in the park.

- **take a picture of** (*fotografini cekmek*): We took a picture of the museum before we went inside.

- **arrive at** (*-e varmak, ulasmak*): They arrived at the museum at 7:30, so it was already closed.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Mektup veya paragrafimda gecmis zaman fiillerini (arrived, saw, went) dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Ziyaret edilen yerleri mantikli bir zaman sirasiyla (Friday, Saturday) anlattim mi?

- [ ] Mektup baslangic ve bitis hitaplarina dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Yazim ve noktalama hatalarini kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 20: Derek

- **ID:** `raw1_020` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Work & Hobbies | **Sayfa:** 22
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_020_derek.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Derek Matthews liked horses, so he wanted to become a ____. *(İpucu: occupation)*

2. Today, at ____, Derek is very famous and owns a riding school. *(İpucu: number)*

3. He drives to Ascot ____ days a week to visit and ride his horses. *(İpucu: number)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What was Derek Matthews like when he was a student?

   - A) He was the best student in his class

   - B) He wasn't a good student at school

   - C) He studied horses at university

   - D) He taught riding lessons while at school

2. Where is Derek's riding school located?

   - A) In central London

   - B) Very far from England

   - C) In Ascot, near London

   - D) Inside a large city school

3. What does Derek do when he goes to Ascot?

   - A) He visits his horses and rides them

   - B) He teaches young children how to drive

   - C) He sells horses to tourists from London

   - D) He studies every evening after work


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Derek Matthews is a famous person today.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Derek Matthews won many horse races as a young jockey.


### 2. Reading

Derek Matthews wasn't a good student at school. He liked horses so he wanted to be a jockey. Today, at 55, he is very famous and has got a riding school in Ascot, a few miles from London. He drives to Ascot three days a week, visits his horses and rides them. He says he enjoys his days at the riding school a lot.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. Why did Derek Matthews want to be a jockey?

2. What does he enjoy?

3. Was he a good student at school?

4. How does he get to Ascot?

5. How far is Ascot (from London)?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the main idea of the passage?

   - A) Derek only drives to Ascot when the weather is good

   - B) Students must study hard to open a riding school

   - C) Derek turned his childhood love for horses into a happy career

   - D) Ascot is the largest city near London

2. *[Inference]* What can we infer about Derek Matthews from the passage?

   - A) He works at the riding school seven days a week

   - B) He dislikes driving to Ascot from London

   - C) He was a teacher before he bought horses

   - D) He probably lives somewhere outside the riding school and travels there

3. *[Reference]* In the sentence "visits his horses and rides them," what does "them" refer to?

   - A) His students

   - B) His horses

   - C) His friends in London

   - D) Three days


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — About Derek Matthews** (50-70 words): Write a short summary of Derek Matthews's life now and in the past.

- **Task 2 — School and Happiness at Work** (80-110 words): Do you think someone needs to be a good student at school to have a happy job in the future? Explain your idea using simple sentences.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **jockey** (*jokey, at yarisi binicisi*): A jockey must be light and know how to ride fast horses.

- **riding school** (*binicilik okulu*): My sister goes to a riding school every Saturday morning.

- **a few miles from** (*birkac mil (uzaklikta)*): The village is only a few miles from the city centre.

- **three days a week** (*haftada uc gun*): She exercises three days a week to stay healthy.

- **enjoy** (*keyif almak, sevmek*): My grandfather really enjoys his quiet mornings in the garden.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Derek hakkinda gecmis ve simdiki zaman cumlelerini ayirt edebildim mi?

- [ ] Kendi hobim veya sevdigim bir is hakkinda basit ve anlasilir cumleler kurdum mu?

- [ ] Ozne-yuklem uyumuna (he drives, he visits, he enjoys) dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Kelime sayisinin hedef aralikta olmasini sagladim mi?

## Passage 21: Roland, Evi, and Della

- **ID:** `raw1_021` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** People & Lifestyles | **Sayfa:** 23
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_021_roland_evi_and_della.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Roland started playing basketball at the age of ____. *(İpucu: number)*

2. Evi plays the ____ in a group called New Band. *(İpucu: musical instrument)*

3. Della Chan has a ____ in Hong Kong. *(İpucu: noun / place)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where do Roland's mother and father live?

   - A) In Berlin

   - B) In New York

   - C) In Athens

   - D) In Hong Kong

2. What job does Evi want to have?

   - A) An engineer

   - B) A restaurant owner

   - C) An English teacher

   - D) A pop singer

3. Where does Evi's band play in the evenings?

   - A) At a university

   - B) At a school

   - C) At a restaurant

   - D) At a cafe


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Roland goes skiing in the Alps every winter.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Evi is married to Luigi.


### 2. Reading

Roland Kohl is an engineer in Berlin, Germany. Roland is a very active person. He is a good basketball player. He started the sport at the age of eleven. And he goes skiing in the Alps every winter. Roland's mother and father live in New York. He was with them last year. Roland wants to live in the States, too.

Evi Pappas is from a small town near Athens and she studies English at university. She wants to be an English teacher. She plays the guitar in a group called New Band. She says, "We play in the evenings at a cafe. Many people like pop music. They come to the cafe and listen to us." Evi isn't married, but she has an Italian boyfriend called Luigi. Evi speaks Greek, English, French, and Italian.

Della Chan is from Hong Kong. She is a student at a school for foreign students in New York. She has a restaurant in Hong Kong. She wants to speak English to the people in her restaurant.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension Questions*

1. What sort of music do New Band play?

2. What spare time activities does Roland do?

3. Which city does Della study English in?

4. Where does Roland live?

5. Who is Luigi?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Why is Della studying English in New York?

   - A) To become an English teacher in Athens

   - B) To speak English with people in her restaurant

   - C) To play music at a cafe in New York

   - D) To visit Roland's parents in New York

2. *[Detail]* How many languages does Evi speak?

   - A) Two

   - B) Three

   - C) Four

   - D) Five

3. *[Inference]* What do Roland and Della have in common?

   - A) Both of them are engineers.

   - B) Both of them play in a pop band.

   - C) Both of them have a connection to New York.

   - D) Both of them are from Hong Kong.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Introducing Three People** (50-70 words): Write a short summary describing Roland, Evi, and Della. Mention where they are from, what they do, and one extra fact about each person.

- **Task 2 — My Life and Plans** (60-90 words): Write a short paragraph about yourself. Include where you live, your job or studies, your spare time activities, the languages you speak, and what you want to do in the future.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **engineer** (*mühendis*): My uncle is an engineer in a big company.

- **active person** (*aktif, hareketli*): She is very active and runs every morning.

- **at the age of** (*... yaşında*): He started swimming at the age of six.

- **go skiing** (*kayağa gitmek*): We go skiing in the mountains every winter.

- **foreign** (*yabancı*): There are many foreign students in our school.

- **pop music** (*pop müzik*): Do you like listening to pop music in the evening?


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Kişilerin mesleklerini, yaşadıkları yerleri ve hobilerini doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Geniş zaman (Simple Present) fiil çekimlerine (-s/-es) dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Şehir, ülke ve dil isimlerini büyük harfle yazdım mı?

- [ ] Cümlelerimi nokta ve virgül kullanarak doğru bağladım mı?

## Passage 22: Letter from Pam

- **ID:** `raw1_022` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Travel & Work | **Sayfa:** 24, 25
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_022_letter_from_pam.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Pam Robinson is from the ____ and works for a big airline company. *(İpucu: country)*

2. In Istanbul, Pam and her colleagues were free for ____ hours. *(İpucu: number)*

3. Pam wants to buy some things for her ____ in Tokyo. *(İpucu: family member)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Where did Pam write her letter from?

   - A) From London

   - B) From Istanbul

   - C) From Tokyo

   - D) From Berlin

2. Why didn't Pam phone her father from Istanbul last month?

   - A) She lost his phone number.

   - B) She didn't have any time.

   - C) The airport phones were broken.

   - D) She was asleep on the plane.

3. What was the food like at the restaurant near the airport?

   - A) Very good and expensive

   - B) Very good and cheap

   - C) Not very good, but cheap

   - D) Not very good and expensive


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Pam visited England, Canada, and Turkey last month.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Pam plans to write her next letter from London.


### 2. Reading

Pam Robinson is a stewardess. She is from the U.S.A. She works for a big airline company. She flies to a lot of countries. She wrote a letter to her father from Tokyo.

Dear Father,

We are in Tokyo today and I'm happy because I can write to you. You know, this is my second month in this job, but I like it very much. I was in a lot of different places last month. I was in England, Canada, and Turkey. I wanted to phone you from Istanbul, but I didn't have any time.

Yesterday we were in Istanbul again. We were free for twelve hours. We didn't want to stay at the airport. First we went to a restaurant near the airport and had lunch because we were very hungry. It wasn't very good, but it was cheap.

Then we went to the city and saw a lot of interesting places, too. The people, the houses, the food! Everything is wonderful in Tokyo, too. I want to buy some things for you here.

I think I've got a wonderful job, but I was really very tired last month.

Wait for my next letter from Berlin. We are going there.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension Questions*

1. When did Pam get this job?

2. Does she like her job?

3. How many countries did she see last month?

4. Did she phone her father from Istanbul?

5. Where was she yesterday?

6. Were they free for lunch in Istanbul?

7. Did they have lunch on the plane?

8. Where was the restaurant?

9. Was the food good?

10. What did they see in Istanbul?

11. How long did they stay in Istanbul?

12. Does she like Tokyo?

13. What does she want to do in Tokyo?

14. Where are they going next?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does "stewardess" mean in the passage?

   - A) A hotel receptionist

   - B) A flight attendant on airplanes

   - C) A tour guide in Tokyo

   - D) A chef at an airport restaurant

2. *[Detail]* Why did Pam leave the airport during her stop in Istanbul?

   - A) Because the airport closed for twelve hours

   - B) Because she had to meet her father in the city

   - C) Because she had free time and wanted to eat and see the city

   - D) Because her plane left for Tokyo immediately

3. *[Inference]* How does Pam feel about her job overall?

   - A) She enjoys it very much, even though it can be tiring.

   - B) She dislikes it because she travels too often.

   - C) She wants to quit because the restaurant food was bad.

   - D) She feels lonely because she never sees interesting places.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary of Pam's Travels** (60-80 words): Summarize Pam's letter to her father in your own words. Mention her job, the places she visited, what she did in Istanbul, and her next destination.

- **Task 2 — Working and Traveling** (80-110 words): Do you think a job that involves a lot of travel is good or difficult? Write a short paragraph giving reasons for your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **stewardess** (*hostes, kabin memuru*): The stewardess gave us water on the plane.

- **airline company** (*hava yolu şirketi*): My sister works for an international airline company.

- **near the airport** (*havalimanının yakınında*): Our hotel is near the airport, so we can walk there.

- **cheap** (*ucuz*): The bus ticket was very cheap compared to the train.

- **interesting places** (*ilginç yerler*): We saw many interesting places during our trip.

- **very tired** (*yorgun*): After flying for ten hours, I felt very tired.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Mektup özetinde geçmiş zaman (Simple Past) fiillerini doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Şehir ve ülke adlarını doğru yazıp büyük harfle başlattım mı?

- [ ] Fikir paragrafımda olumlu ve olumsuz yönleri dengeli şekilde açıkladım mı?

- [ ] Kelime sayısı hedefine uydum mu ve bağlaçları (but, because, and) doğru kullandım mı?

## Passage 23: Joan

- **ID:** `raw1_023` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Career & Computers | **Sayfa:** 26
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_023_joan.mp3` (*US · en-US-JennyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Computers were very ____ for Joan when she was at school. *(İpucu: adjective)*

2. Joan finished school ____ years ago. *(İpucu: number)*

3. Joan can draw ____ with computers. *(İpucu: plural noun)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What kind of student was Joan at school?

   - A) A lazy student

   - B) A very good student

   - C) A poor student

   - D) An average student

2. Where does Joan work now?

   - A) At a school

   - B) At a hospital

   - C) In an office

   - D) In a factory


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Joan started working as a computer programmer two years ago.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Joan does not enjoy working in the office.


### 2. Reading

Joan was a very good student when she was at school. Computers were very interesting for her, so she wanted to work with computers. She finished school two years ago and started to work as a computer programmer. Now she enjoys the work in the office very much. She can draw pictures with computers.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension Questions*

1. Why did Joan want to work with computers?

2. What does she enjoy?

3. When did she start to work as a computer programmer?

4. What can she do with computers?

5. Ask and answer if Joan was a good student at school.


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* What is Joan's job now?

   - A) A school teacher

   - B) A computer programmer

   - C) A painter

   - D) A school student

2. *[Detail]* What happened after Joan finished school two years ago?

   - A) She stopped using computers.

   - B) She began working as a computer programmer.

   - C) She went back to school to study art.

   - D) She opened a computer shop.

3. *[Inference]* How does Joan feel about her current job?

   - A) She thinks it is boring.

   - B) She wants to leave the office.

   - C) She likes it very much.

   - D) She finds it too hard.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — About Joan** (45-65 words): Summarize Joan's story in your own words. Mention what she was like at school, her job, and what she can do now.

- **Task 2 — My Interests and Future Job** (60-85 words): Write a short paragraph about a school subject you find interesting and the job you want to do in the future.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **good student** (*iyi öğrenci*): Ali was a good student and always did his homework.

- **interesting** (*ilginç*): This book about space is very interesting.

- **finish school** (*okulu bitirmek*): My brother will finish school next June.

- **computer programmer** (*bilgisayar programcısı*): She works as a computer programmer for a tech firm.

- **enjoy the work** (*işinden keyif almak*): I really enjoy my work in the library.

- **draw pictures** (*resim çizmek*): The children can draw pictures of animals.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Geçmiş zaman (was, wanted, finished, started) ve şimdiki/geniş zaman (enjoys, can draw) ayrımını doğru yaptım mı?

- [ ] Cümlelerimde noktalama ve büyük harf kurallarına dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Fikirlerimi so ve because bağlaçlarıyla mantıklı şekilde birleştirdim mi?

- [ ] İstenen kelime sayısına yaklaştım mı?

## Passage 24: Mahatma Gandhi

- **ID:** `raw1_024` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** History & Biography | **Sayfa:** 27
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_024_mahatma_gandhi.mp3` (*AU · en-AU-NatashaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Mahatma Gandhi was born in India in ____. *(İpucu: year)*

2. Gandhi went to London and became a ____. *(İpucu: profession)*

3. After returning to India, Gandhi worked hard to help the ____. *(İpucu: noun / group of people)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. How old was Gandhi when he got married?

   - A) Thirteen

   - B) Eighteen

   - C) Twenty

   - D) Thirty

2. When did Gandhi return to India from South Africa?

   - A) Two years later

   - B) Ten years later

   - C) Twenty years later

   - D) Thirty years later

3. In what year was Gandhi killed?

   - A) In 1869

   - B) In 1920

   - C) In 1948

   - D) In 1969


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Life was easy for Gandhi when he was young.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Gandhi played a very important role in India's independence.


### 2. Reading

Mahatma Gandhi was born in India in 1869. He became one of his country's leaders. Life was difficult when he was young. He got married at the age of thirteen; a common custom in India at that time. He studied law at the university. He went to London and became a lawyer. Then he returned to his country. A few years later, he went to work in South Africa. He organised demonstrations and they sent him to prison several times. Twenty years later, he returned to India. There, he worked hard to help the poor. He played a very important role in the independence of India. A fanatic killed him in 1948.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Comprehension Questions*

1. Where was Gandhi born?

2. When was he born?

3. Why did he get married at the age of thirteen?

4. Why did he go to London?

5. Where did he organise demonstrations?

6. When did he return to India?

7. Who did he help in India?

8. Who killed him?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* What happened to Gandhi while he was working in South Africa?

   - A) He got married at age thirteen.

   - B) He studied law at a university.

   - C) He organised demonstrations and went to prison several times.

   - D) He became the king of South Africa.

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does "custom" mean in "a common custom in India at that time"?

   - A) A traditional social practice

   - B) A university rule

   - C) A government law

   - D) A type of protest

3. *[Main Idea]* What is the main topic of the passage?

   - A) The education system in London

   - B) The history of South African prisons

   - C) The life and leadership of Mahatma Gandhi

   - D) Wedding traditions in modern India


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Life of Mahatma Gandhi** (65-85 words): Write a short biography summarizing the main events in Mahatma Gandhi's life based on the passage.

- **Task 2 — What Makes a Great Leader?** (80-110 words): What qualities make someone a great leader? Write a short paragraph explaining your opinion with at least one example.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **common custom** (*gelenek, adet*): Drinking tea in the morning is a common custom in Turkey.

- **lawyer** (*avukat*): She studied hard at university to become a lawyer.

- **demonstration** (*gösteri, protesto yürüyüşü*): Hundreds of people joined the peaceful demonstration in the square.

- **prison** (*hapis, cezaevi*): The police sent the thief to prison for two years.

- **the poor** (*yoksullar, fakirler*): The charity collects food and clothes to help the poor.

- **independence** (*bağımsızlık*): The country celebrated its independence in 1923.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Tarihleri ve geçmiş zaman fiillerini (was born, became, studied, returned) doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Olayları kronolojik sıraya göre (doğumundan ölümüne kadar) anlattım mı?

- [ ] Görüş paragrafımda bir liderde olması gereken özellikleri örneklerle açıkladım mı?

- [ ] Özel isimleri ve ülke adlarını büyük harfle yazdım mı?

## Passage 25: William Shakespeare

- **ID:** `raw1_025` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Literature & Biography | **Sayfa:** 28
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_025_william_shakespeare.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. In 1587, William Shakespeare went to London and worked as an actor in a theatre called ____. *(İpucu: theatre name)*

2. He began to write plays for the ____, and everyone liked his plays. *(İpucu: plural noun)*

3. When Shakespeare died in 1610, he was only ____ years old. *(İpucu: number)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What do we learn about William Shakespeare's family background?

   - A) He came from a very poor family.

   - B) His father was an important man in town.

   - C) He moved to London when he was eighteen.

   - D) His family owned a theatre in Stratford.

2. What happened to the Globe Theatre during Shakespeare's last play?

   - A) It closed when King James the First died.

   - B) It was sold to another acting company.

   - C) A fire from a stage gun burned it down.

   - D) It was destroyed during a storm in 1610.

3. Why did Shakespeare move to London in 1587?

   - A) To become an actor at the best theatres

   - B) To marry Anne Hathaway

   - C) To escape his family in Stratford

   - D) To study under King James the First


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — William Shakespeare met and married Anne Hathaway after moving to London.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Shakespeare performed his plays for King James the First and his friends.


### 2. Reading

William Shakespeare was born in Stratford on April 23rd, 1564. His father, John Shakespeare, was an important man in the town - William did not come from a poor family.

When he was eighteen, William married Anne Hathaway in Stratford. But he didn't want to stay there. He wanted to be an actor and the best theatres were in London. So, in 1587, William went to London, where he worked as an actor in a theatre called The Rose.

He began to write plays for the actors. He wrote Romeo and Juliet, Julius Caesar, Richard II, Antony and Cleopatra and many more. Everyone liked his plays, and he became famous.

When James I became King in 1603, Shakespeare worked for him, and performed his plays for the King and his friends. He also worked at the famous Globe Theatre. This theatre presented his last play, Henry VIII. There was a gun in this play, and fire from the gun burned the theatre down.

In 1610 Shakespeare went back to Stratford. He wanted to live there with his family, but he died on April 23rd, 1610. He was only forty-six years old.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A*

1. How old was Shakespeare when he got married?

2. What was his wife's name?

3. Why did Shakespeare go to London?

4. What was the name of the first theatre he worked in?

5. What was he famous for?


*B*

6. Fill in what happened in Shakespeare's life in 1564 (write only one thing): 1564 — ____

7. Fill in what happened in Shakespeare's life in 1582 (write only one thing): 1582 — ____

8. Fill in what happened in Shakespeare's life in 1587 (write only one thing): 1587 — ____

9. Fill in what happened in Shakespeare's life in 1603 (write only one thing): 1603 — ____

10. Fill in what happened in Shakespeare's life in 1610 (write only one thing): 1610 — ____


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the passage mainly about?

   - A) The history of London's oldest theatres

   - B) How King James I supported theatre actors

   - C) Key events in William Shakespeare's life and career

   - D) Why Stratford was a bad place for actors in the 1500s

2. *[Inference]* Which of the following can be inferred from the passage?

   - A) Shakespeare stopped writing plays as soon as James I became King.

   - B) Shakespeare worked at more than one theatre during his career in London.

   - C) Shakespeare's father did not want him to become an actor.

   - D) Shakespeare lived in London until the end of his life.

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In paragraph 4, the word "presented" is closest in meaning to ____.

   - A) bought

   - B) described

   - C) published

   - D) staged


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: William Shakespeare's Life** (60-80 words): Write a short summary of William Shakespeare's life using the key dates and events from the passage.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Moving to a Big City for Success** (80-110 words): Shakespeare left his small hometown of Stratford to work in London. Do you think moving to a big city is necessary to become successful? Give reasons for your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **be born** (*doğmak*): My grandfather was born in a small village near Izmir.

- **stay** (*kalmak*): She wants to stay here until the end of the summer.

- **perform** (*sahnelemek, icra etmek*): The band performed their new songs for a huge crowd.

- **burn down** (*yanıp kül olmak*): The old wooden building burned down last night.

- **go back** (*geri dönmek*): After studying abroad, he went back to his hometown.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Geçmiş zaman (Past Simple) fiillerini doğru çekimledim mi?

- [ ] Shakespeare'in hayatındaki önemli tarihleri ve yerleri doğru sıraladım mı?

- [ ] Fikirlerimi basit bağlaçlarla (and, but, because, so) birbirine bağladım mı?

- [ ] Kelime sayısını hedeflenen aralıkta tuttum ve yazım hatası yapmadım mı?

## Passage 26: Alfred Hitchcock

- **ID:** `raw1_026` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Cinema & Biography | **Sayfa:** 29
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_026_alfred_hitchcock.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Alfred Hitchcock and his wife Alma Revelle had one ____. *(İpucu: noun)*

2. In 1939, he left Britain because he wanted to work in ____. *(İpucu: place)*

3. Queen Elizabeth the Second made Hitchcock 'Sir Alfred' in ____. *(İpucu: year)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What happened in Alfred Hitchcock's life in 1920?

   - A) He moved to Los Angeles.

   - B) He directed Psycho.

   - C) He got married and began working for a film company.

   - D) He became 'Sir Alfred'.

2. How many films did Alfred Hitchcock make in total?

   - A) Exactly 39 films

   - B) 53 films

   - C) 60 films

   - D) 13 films

3. Where and when did Alfred Hitchcock die?

   - A) London in 1963

   - B) Hollywood in 1939

   - C) London in 1980

   - D) Los Angeles in 1980


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Alfred Hitchcock made his famous film Psycho in Britain in the 1930s.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Hitchcock stayed married to Alma Revelle for sixty years.


### 2. Reading

Alfred Joseph Hitchcock was born in London on August 13, 1899. He went to school and university there and in 1920 he started to work with a British film company. In 1920 he also married Alma Revelle, his wife for the next sixty years. They had one daughter.

From 1925 to 1939 he made several films in Britain, including The 39 Steps and The Man Who Knew Too Much.

In 1939 he left Britain because he wanted to work in Hollywood. He lived in Los Angeles for the rest of his life and made many of his 53 films there. A lot of people in America and Europe saw his famous films Psycho (1960) and The Birds (1963).

Queen Elizabeth II made Hitchcock 'Sir Alfred' in 1980. Soon after that, in May 1980, he died in Los Angeles.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Main*

1. Where did Hitchcock go to school and university?

2. What happened in 1920?

3. How many children did he have?

4. What are the names of his famous films between the years 1925-1939?

5. Where did he make most of his films?

6. What are the names of his two famous films?

7. Who made him 'Sir Alfred'?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Which of the following is supported by the passage?

   - A) He spent a longer part of his career working in the USA than in Britain.

   - B) He stopped making films after 1963 because of his health.

   - C) His films were only popular in Los Angeles.

   - D) He returned to London in 1980 to receive a title from Queen Elizabeth II.

2. *[Reference]* In paragraph 1, what does "there" refer to in "He went to school and university there"?

   - A) Hollywood

   - B) Los Angeles

   - C) London

   - D) a British film company

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In paragraph 3, the phrase "for the rest of his life" means ____.

   - A) for a few years before returning to Britain

   - B) until he died in 1980

   - C) only while filming Psycho and The Birds

   - D) until he married Alma Revelle


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: The Career of Alfred Hitchcock** (60-80 words): Write a brief biography of Alfred Hitchcock based on the passage, mentioning both his early career in Britain and his later years in the USA.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Your Favourite Type of Film** (80-110 words): Alfred Hitchcock was famous for suspense and thriller films. What kind of films do you enjoy watching most, and why? Give an example of a film you like.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **start to work with** (*... ile çalışmaya başlamak / işe başlamak*): She started to work at a software company after university.

- **several** (*birkaç, çeşitli*): We visited several museums during our trip to Rome.

- **including** (*dahil, ... dahil olmak üzere*): The menu includes several vegetarian dishes, including lentil soup.

- **for the rest of his life** (*hayatının geri kalanı boyunca*): They decided to stay in the village for the rest of their lives.

- **soon after that** (*bundan kısa bir süre sonra*): Soon after that, the rain stopped and the sun came out.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Büyük harf kurallarına (yer adları, film adları ve kişi isimleri) dikkat ettim mi?

- [ ] Hitchcock'un İngiltere ve ABD'deki dönemlerini net bir şekilde ayırdım mı?

- [ ] Tarihlerden ve yerlerden bahsederken doğru edatları (in, on, from... to) kullandım mı?

- [ ] Cümlelerim açık ve hedeflenen kelime sayısına uygun mu?

## Passage 27: Charlie Chaplin

- **ID:** `raw1_027` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Cinema & Biography | **Sayfa:** 30
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_027_charlie_chaplin.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Charlie Chaplin first appeared in ____ and then acted on the London stage. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

2. He made his first film, Making A Living, in ____. *(İpucu: year)*

3. After leaving the USA in 1952, Chaplin lived with his family in ____ for the rest of his life. *(İpucu: country)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What did Charlie Chaplin do in 1913?

   - A) He joined Fred Karno's company in London.

   - B) He retired from acting on the London stage.

   - C) He started working at the Keystone Company in Hollywood.

   - D) He moved to Switzerland with his family.

2. What made Charlie Chaplin famous?

   - A) Making A Living

   - B) the little tramp

   - C) Fred Karno

   - D) Keystone Company

3. When did Charlie Chaplin marry Oona O'Neill?

   - A) 1943

   - B) 1942

   - C) 1952

   - D) 1954


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Charlie Chaplin worked with Fred Karno's company in the USA for many years.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Charlie Chaplin left the United States because of political reasons.


### 2. Reading

Charlie Chaplin was born in London in 1889. He first appeared in music halls and then acted on the London stage. In 1910 he went to the USA with Fred Karno's company, but he didn't stay with Karno very long. In 1913 he started to work at the Keystone Company in Hollywood. There he made his first film, Making A Living, in 1914. In his second film, Kid Auto Races at Venice (1914), he introduced the little tramp. This character made him famous.

Chaplin got married several times. He met his last wife, Oona O'Neill in 1942 and married her a year later. Chaplin was then 54 years old and Oona 18. He left the USA in 1952 for political reasons and lived with his family in Switzerland for the rest of his life. He died in 1977.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Main*

1. Where was Charlie Chaplin born?

2. When was he born?

3. Where did he first appear?

4. Where did he go in 1910?

5. What did he do in 1913?

6. When did he make his first film?

7. What character did he introduce in his second film?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* What does paragraph 1 tell us about Chaplin's early career?

   - A) Chaplin was already famous in Switzerland before 1910.

   - B) Chaplin had stage acting experience before he began making films.

   - C) Chaplin made his first film with Fred Karno's company.

   - D) Chaplin's first film was more famous than his second film.

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In paragraph 1, the word "introduced" is closest in meaning to ____.

   - A) forgot about

   - B) copied from another actor

   - C) stopped playing

   - D) presented for the first time

3. *[Inference]* Which statement can be inferred from paragraph 2?

   - A) Chaplin spent the last twenty-five years of his life in Switzerland.

   - B) Oona O'Neill was Chaplin's first and only wife.

   - C) Chaplin returned to Hollywood shortly before he died in 1977.

   - D) Chaplin stopped working because he met Oona O'Neill.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: Charlie Chaplin's Life** (60-80 words): Summarize Charlie Chaplin's life and career in your own words, covering his early work, his rise to fame, and his later life.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Are Silent Comedies Still Funny?** (80-110 words): Charlie Chaplin became famous in silent films without spoken dialogue. Do you think silent comedy films can still be funny and popular today? Why or why not?


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **appear** (*sahneye çıkmak, görünmek*): The young singer first appeared on television in 2018.

- **introduce** (*tanıtmak, ilk kez sunmak*): The author introduced a new hero in her second novel.

- **get married** (*evlenmek*): My grandparents got married in a small town in 1975.

- **for political reasons** (*siyasi nedenlerle*): She left her country for political reasons.

- **stage** (*sahne, tiyatro sahnesi*): He acted on stage for ten years before making films.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Chaplin'in sahne ve sinema kariyeri arasındaki geçişi doğru aktardım mı?

- [ ] Tarihleri ve olayları kronolojik bir sırayla yazdım mı?

- [ ] Geçmiş zaman yapılarını (was born, appeared, went, died) hatasız kullandım mı?

- [ ] Hedeflenen kelime sınırına uydum mu?

## Passage 28: Super Tanker Disaster

- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_028_super_tanker_disaster.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. There was a bad accident in the ____ at about two in the morning. *(İpucu: place)*

2. An English oil tanker crashed into an Italian ____. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

3. They say that the accident happened because it was very ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. When did the accident happen?

   - A) At two in the afternoon

   - B) At about two in the morning

   - C) At eight in the evening

   - D) At midnight

2. Which two ships crashed into each other?

   - A) Two Italian oil tankers

   - B) An English cargo ship and an Italian ferry

   - C) An English oil tanker and an Italian cargo ship

   - D) A French passenger ship and an oil tanker

3. Why do they say the accident happened?

   - A) Because of very stormy weather

   - B) Because the ship was too old

   - C) Because the captain was asleep

   - D) Because there were too many ships


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — A French oil tanker crashed into an Italian cargo ship.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Several sailors were injured in the crash.


### 2. Reading

Daily News

There was a bad accident in the English Channel at about two this morning. An English oil tanker crashed into an Italian cargo ship. They say that the accident happened because it was very stormy... (more on page 8)


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Main*

1. What time did the accident happen?

2. What was the weather like when the accident happened?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose / Text Type]* What kind of text is this?

   - A) A personal letter to a friend

   - B) A short newspaper report

   - C) A weather forecast for next week

   - D) An advertisement for a boat trip

2. *[Detail]* Which statement is true according to the text?

   - A) Only one ship was damaged.

   - B) The accident happened on land near a port.

   - C) Both ships came from Italy.

   - D) Two ships from different countries were involved in the crash.

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does "stormy" mean in this report?

   - A) warm and sunny

   - B) quiet and foggy

   - C) windy and rough with bad weather

   - D) freezing with heavy snow


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Short Response: News Summary** (40-60 words): Write a short news summary about the accident in the English Channel using your own words.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Travel Safety in Bad Weather** (60-90 words): Why is bad weather dangerous for ships and cars? Write a short paragraph about how people can stay safe in stormy weather.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **accident** (*kaza*): There was a small car accident near the school yesterday.

- **crash into** (*çarpmak*): The bus crashed into a tree on the icy road.

- **oil tanker** (*petrol tankeri*): An oil tanker carries thousands of tons of fuel across the sea.

- **cargo ship** (*yük gemisi*): A large cargo ship arrived at the port this morning.

- **stormy** (*fırtınalı*): We stayed inside all day because the weather was stormy.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Haberin ne zaman, nerede ve nasıl olduğunu net bir şekilde yazdım mı?

- [ ] Basit geçmiş zaman (was, crashed, happened) fiillerini doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Gemilerin milliyetlerini ve türlerini (English oil tanker, Italian cargo ship) doğru yazdım mı?

- [ ] Kısa ve anlaşılır cümleler kurdum mu?

## Passage 29: Selton Times

- **ID:** `raw1_029` | **Seviye:** `A1` | **Konu:** Shopping & Local News | **Sayfa:** 32
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_029_selton_times.mp3` (*US · en-US-JennyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. The new shopping centre is a ____ building. *(İpucu: adjective)*

2. On weekdays, the stores close at ____. *(İpucu: time)*

3. The entire shopping centre is ____ on Sundays. *(İpucu: adjective)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What is true about the new shopping centre?

   - A) It only sells books and shoes.

   - B) It has stores for clothes and shoes, plus cafes and restaurants.

   - C) It is open seven days a week.

   - D) It has three floors for shopping and dining.

2. What are the opening hours for cafes and restaurants on Saturday?

   - A) 7 am to 8 pm

   - B) 7 am to 9 pm

   - C) 7 am to 10 pm

   - D) 8 am to 10 pm


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Selton's second shopping centre opened on Wednesday, February 16th.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — On weekdays, the cafes and restaurants stay open later than the stores.


### 2. Reading

WEDNESDAY, FEBRUARY 16th, No. 532

SELTON'S SECOND SHOPPING CENTRE OPENED YESTERDAY!

The four-floor building has everything from books to shoes. You can find fashionable clothes for your family and friends. You can have breakfast, lunch, and dinner at the lovely cafes and restaurants.

Selton Shopping Centre
Cafes & Restaurants open: Monday - Friday (7 am - 9 pm) / Saturday (7 am - 10 pm)
Stores open: Monday - Friday (7 am - 8 pm) / Saturday (7 am - 10 pm)
Centre closed on Sundays.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Questions*

1. 1. What can you buy at the new shopping centre? (Write two things.)

2. 2. Is there only one shopping centre in Selton?

3. 3. Can you have dinner in the shopping centre on Sunday?

4. 4. Are the stores open at 9 am on Saturday?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of this newspaper article?

   - A) To advertise a sale on books and shoes

   - B) To complain about Sunday closing hours

   - C) To announce a new shopping centre and share its opening hours

   - D) To compare two shopping centres in Selton

2. *[Inference]* What can be inferred from the opening schedule?

   - A) Stores and cafes always close at the same time.

   - B) Cafes stay open one hour later than stores on weekdays.

   - C) Stores are open longer than cafes on Saturdays.

   - D) The centre opens later on Saturdays than on weekdays.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: New Shopping Centre** (60-80 words): Write a short summary of the news about the new shopping centre in Selton. Include what people can find there and its opening hours.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Shopping Centres** (70-100 words): Do you like shopping at big shopping centres? Why or why not? Write a short paragraph giving your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **shopping centre** (*alışveriş merkezi*): We visited the new shopping centre on Saturday.

- **four-floor** (*dört katlı*): She works in a four-floor office building.

- **fashionable** (*moda, şık*): He always wears fashionable clothes to school.

- **lovely** (*hoş, güzel*): There are many lovely cafes in our neighbourhood.

- **closed** (*kapalı*): The museum is closed on Mondays.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Pazar günleri merkezin kapalı olduğunu ekledim mi?

- [ ] Basit zaman kiplerini (Present Simple / Past Simple) ve noktalama işaretlerini kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 30: Lebrat's Visit

- **ID:** `raw1_030` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Business & Travel | **Sayfa:** 33
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_030_lebrat_s_visit.mp3` (*AU · en-AU-NatashaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Christian Lebrat is the ____ of the MAGROS Food company. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

2. He first visited Selton in ____ to open the Oxham Street store. *(İpucu: year)*

3. Tomorrow morning, Mr. Lebrat is flying to ____ to visit the stores there. *(İpucu: city)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. Why is Christian Lebrat visiting Selton?

   - A) To open a new store in Selton

   - B) To meet the manager of the local MAGROS store

   - C) To move the company headquarters to England

   - D) To celebrate the opening of a store in Rome

2. Which European country currently has the largest number of MAGROS stores?

   - A) France

   - B) Spain

   - C) England

   - D) Germany

3. Where does Mr. Lebrat plan to open a store next year?

   - A) Rome, Italy

   - B) Paris, France

   - C) Berlin, Germany

   - D) London, England


**C. True / False / Not Given**

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Mr. Lebrat has a busy schedule during his trip.


### 2. Reading

Christian Lebrat, the French president of the MAGROS Food company, is visiting our town. He arrived yesterday and he is here to meet Mr. Douglas, the manager of the MAGROS store in Oxham Street. This is Mr. Lebrat's second visit to Selton. He was here in 1991 when he opened the store.

There are sixty-five MAGROS stores in Europe - twenty in England, twelve in Germany, fifteen in France and eighteen in Spain. Next year, Mr. Lebrat wants to open a store in Rome, Italy, too. Mr. Lebrat can't stay in Selton for very long. He has a busy programme. He is flying to Madrid tomorrow morning to visit the stores there.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A. Mark the statements as True (T) or False (F).*

1. 1. Mr. Lebrat is from Spain.

   - True

   - False

2. 2. He came to Selton on Thursday.

   - True

   - False

3. 3. Mr. Lebrat is in Selton because he wants to open a new store.

   - True

   - False

4. 4. The MAGROS Store in Oxham Street opened in 1991.

   - True

   - False

5. 5. The MAGROS Store in Selton has got many managers.

   - True

   - False

6. 6. Mr. Lebrat is leaving Selton on Wednesday morning.

   - True

   - False

7. 7. There are some MAGROS stores in Madrid.

   - True

   - False


*B. Fill in the chart with numbers according to the text. (Europe: 65)*

8. The number of MAGROS stores in England: ____

9. The number of MAGROS stores in Germany: ____

10. The number of MAGROS stores in France: ____

11. The number of MAGROS stores in Spain: ____

12. The number of MAGROS stores in Italy: ____


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* What do we learn about Mr. Lebrat's future business plans?

   - A) He is closing stores in Spain and Germany.

   - B) He plans to expand the company into Italy next year.

   - C) He has already opened stores in Rome.

   - D) He wants to sell the store in Oxham Street.

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* What does the word 'programme' most likely mean in the passage?

   - A) a television show

   - B) a computer application

   - C) a printed booklet

   - D) a planned schedule


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary: Mr. Lebrat's Visit** (60-85 words): Summarize Christian Lebrat's visit to Selton and the information about the MAGROS Food company in your own words.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: International Stores** (80-110 words): Do you think it is good for a town to have international food stores? Write a short opinion paragraph explaining your reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **president** (*başkan, yönetici*): She became the president of a large company last year.

- **manager** (*müdür, yönetici*): Please ask the store manager if you need help.

- **open a store** (*mağaza açmak*): They are planning to open a new branch next summer.

- **busy programme** (*yoğun program*): I have a very busy programme this week.

- **arrive** (*varmak, ulaşmak*): Our flight arrived in Madrid early in the morning.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Yazım ve dil bilgisi hatalarını kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 31: The Buzz Survey

- **ID:** `raw1_031` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Youth Reading Habits | **Sayfa:** 34
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_031_the_buzz_survey.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-SoniaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. The Buzz is a ____ magazine in England. *(İpucu: adjective)*

2. Paul enjoys reading ____ because he finds them very interesting. *(İpucu: noun phrase)*

3. Tom only reads magazines when he is at the ____. *(İpucu: place)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What kind of books does Jenny read?

   - A) Detective stories

   - B) Love stories

   - C) School books

   - D) Daily newspapers

2. Why does Eric refuse to read The Sun?

   - A) It is too expensive for students.

   - B) His father already reads it at home.

   - C) It only publishes stories for women.

   - D) He thinks it has no real news in it.

3. Which student reads newspapers almost every day?

   - A) Paul

   - B) Eric

   - C) Jenny

   - D) Tom


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Paul prefers detective stories to love stories.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tom spends a lot of money buying magazines every week.


### 2. Reading

What do young British people read? Books? Magazines? Newspapers? The Buzz - a weekly magazine in England - interviewed some students and asked them questions about what they read. Here are some of the responses.

What do the British read?
Paul, 17, said, "I like reading detective stories. They are very interesting. I hate love stories. I think they are for women."
Jenny, 16, "I don't buy any books because I don't like reading books at all. I only read my school books. That's all."

Do they read newspapers?
Eric, 17, "Yes, I usually read the newspaper every day. My father buys The Daily Mirror. I sometimes buy The Times or The Guardian. I never read The Sun because there isn't any news in it."

And what about magazines?
Tom, "I don't buy magazines but I always read them when I'm at the doctor's office."


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Mark the statements as True (T) or False (F).*

1. 1. The Buzz is a newspaper.

   - True

   - False

2. 2. Paul doesn't mind reading love stories.

   - True

   - False

3. 3. Jenny enjoys reading.

   - True

   - False

4. 4. Eric's father reads The Daily Mirror.

   - True

   - False

5. 5. Eric thinks The Sun is a good newspaper.

   - True

   - False

6. 6. Tom always buys magazines and reads them in his office.

   - True

   - False

7. 7. There are magazines at Tom's doctor's office.

   - True

   - False


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the main purpose of this passage?

   - A) To share the results of a survey on young people's reading habits

   - B) To encourage teenagers to buy more magazines

   - C) To criticize British newspapers for poor reporting

   - D) To explain why students dislike school books

2. *[Inference]* What can be inferred about Tom's reading habits?

   - A) He reads magazines every morning before school.

   - B) He works part-time at a doctor's office.

   - C) He only reads magazines while waiting at the doctor's office.

   - D) He prefers magazines to detective stories.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary: Student Reading Habits** (65-90 words): Summarize the reading habits of the four students interviewed by The Buzz magazine.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: My Reading Habits** (80-110 words): What do you like or dislike reading in your free time? Compare your habits with one of the students in the survey.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **weekly magazine** (*haftalık dergi*): This weekly magazine has interesting articles for teenagers.

- **interview** (*röportaj yapmak, görüşmek*): The journalist interviewed five students after class.

- **response** (*yanıt, cevap*): We received many helpful responses to our survey.

- **detective story** (*polisiye hikaye*): My brother enjoys reading detective stories before bed.

- **not ... at all** (*hiç (olumsuz cümlede)*): I don't like watching horror films at all.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Ankette yer alan dört öğrencinin (Paul, Jenny, Eric, Tom) okuma alışkanlıklarına değindim mi?

- [ ] Beğenme ve beğenmeme ifadelerini (like, hate, don't like at all) doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Gazete ve dergi isimlerini büyük harfle ve doğru yazdım mı?

- [ ] Kendi görüşümü ifade ederken net nedenler sundum mu?

## Passage 32: D.H. Lawrence

- **ID:** `raw1_032` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Literature & Biography | **Sayfa:** 35
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_032_d_h_lawrence.mp3` (*US · en-US-GuyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. D.H. Lawrence was born in ____ in England. *(İpucu: year)*

2. He and his wife bought a house near ____ in Germany. *(İpucu: city)*

3. His famous book The Rainbow was published in ____. *(İpucu: year)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. How many children were in D.H. Lawrence's family, including himself?

   - A) Four children

   - B) Five children

   - C) Three children

   - D) Six children

2. In which year did D.H. Lawrence get married?

   - A) 1910

   - B) 1913

   - C) 1914

   - D) 1920

3. Which of the following is one of D.H. Lawrence's famous books mentioned in the passage?

   - A) Women in Love

   - B) Nottingham Lives

   - C) Munich Days

   - D) Brothers and Sisters


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — D.H. Lawrence and his wife lived in Germany for ten years.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — D.H. Lawrence and Frieda had two children.


### 2. Reading

D.H. Lawrence was an English writer. He was born in 1885, in England. He had two brothers and two sisters. He lived in Nottingham and went to school there. He loved his mother very much, but she died in 1910, when Lawrence was 25.

Lawrence married Frieda Van Richthofen in 1914. They lived in England for a short time and later they moved to Germany. They bought a house near Munich and stayed there for two years. He wrote many books. Sons and Lovers (1913), The Rainbow (1915) and Women in Love (1920) are some of his famous books. He died in 1930.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*Mark the statements as True (T), False (F) or No Information (NI).*

1. 1. D.H. Lawrence was American.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

2. 2. He went to school in Nottingham.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

3. 3. Frieda was Lawrence's mother.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

4. 4. Lawrence's father died in 1915.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

5. 5. Lawrence wrote 'Sons and Lovers' in 1913.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

6. 6. Lawrence died in Germany.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Detail]* Approximately how old was D.H. Lawrence when he died?

   - A) About 25 years old

   - B) About 35 years old

   - C) About 45 years old

   - D) About 55 years old

2. *[Inference]* Which statement accurately describes the order of events in D.H. Lawrence's life?

   - A) He moved to Munich before his mother died.

   - B) He wrote Sons and Lovers before he married Frieda.

   - C) He wrote Women in Love after 1930.

   - D) He went to school in Germany.


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Biography Summary: D.H. Lawrence** (65-85 words): Write a brief biography summarizing the main events of D.H. Lawrence's life based on the passage.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Travel and Writing** (80-110 words): How do you think living in another country can help a writer? Write a short paragraph explaining your opinion.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **be born** (*doğmak*): My grandfather was born in a small village in 1950.

- **marry** (*evlenmek*): He married his university friend in 2018.

- **move to** (*taşınmak*): They moved to another city for a new job.

- **near** (*yakınında*): We stayed in a quiet hotel near the mountains.

- **famous** (*ünlü, tanınmış*): Istanbul is famous for its historic buildings.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] D.H. Lawrence'ın doğum, aile ve eğitim bilgilerini kronolojik sırayla verdim mi?

- [ ] Evliliği, Almanya'ya taşınması ve ünlü kitaplarının isimlerini doğru yazdım mı?

- [ ] Geçmiş zaman (Past Simple) fiil çekimlerini (was, had, died, married, wrote) doğru kullandım mı?

- [ ] Metinde yer almayan bilgileri uydurmadan yalnızca verilen gerçeklere sadık kaldım mı?

## Passage 33: The Funniest Film

- **ID:** `raw1_033` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Cinema & Humor | **Sayfa:** 35, 36
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_033_the_funniest_film.mp3` (*US · en-US-AriaNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. The man bought ____ tickets before going inside the cinema. *(İpucu: number)*

2. Their seats were located in the ____, at the side. *(İpucu: noun)*

3. The man was surprised by the dog's reaction because the dog ____ the book. *(İpucu: verb)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What amazed the usherette most during the film?

   - A) The man brought popcorn for his dog

   - B) The dog fell asleep during the film

   - C) The dog watched the film closely and cried when the hero died

   - D) The usherette had seen the same dog the day before

2. When did the usherette talk to the man?

   - A) Before the film started

   - B) After the film finished

   - C) Right when the hero died

   - D) While she was showing them to their seats

3. Why did the man say he could not understand his dog's reaction?

   - A) The dog had already seen the film twice

   - B) The dog always cried at loud noises

   - C) The film was based on a true story about dogs

   - D) The dog had hated the book the film was based on


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The man and his dog sat in the front row in the middle of the cinema.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — The dog started crying when the main character died in the film.


### 2. Reading

One day a man took his dog to the cinema. He bought two tickets and they went inside. The usherette was very surprised, but she showed them to their places. Their seats were in the stalls, at the side. The usherette was even more surprised when the film began and the dog watched it. He was obviously interested in it. But when the hero died tragically, she was completely amazed; the dog began to cry! He cried like a baby.

After the film, she spoke to the man. "Excuse me, but I've never seen a dog cry in a cinema before."

"Well, it was a very sad film," the man replied.

"But your dog was so interested in the film. It's the most amazing thing I've ever seen."

The man nodded, "Yes, it's very surprising. To tell the truth, I can't understand it. He hated the book."


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A*

1. A man took his dog to the cinema because

   - a) he bought two tickets

   - b) he didn't like going alone

   - c) the dog liked watching films

   - d) he didn't want to leave his dog at home

2. The usherette was very surprised because

   - a) the dog sat near the man

   - b) the hero died tragically

   - c) the man and his dog went inside

   - d) the dog watched the film and cried

3. The usherette spoke to the man because

   - a) the man was very interested in the film

   - b) the man wanted to take his dog inside

   - c) the dog cried when the hero died

   - d) the film was very sad

4. Choose the statement that is true according to the passage.

   - a) The dog liked the film but hated the book.

   - b) The dog liked the book but hated the film.

   - c) The dog watched the film but didn't read the book.

   - d) The dog didn't cry when he read the book.


*B*

5. What does 'they' refer to in "He bought two tickets and they went inside."?

6. What does 'she' refer to in "The usherette was very surprised, but she showed them to their places."?

7. What does 'He' refer to in "when the film began and the dog watched it. He was obviously interested in it."?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Author's Purpose]* What is the main purpose of this passage?

   - A) To explain how animals can be trained for cinema roles

   - B) To criticize the quality of modern film adaptations

   - C) To tell a humorous story with an unexpected punchline

   - D) To warn cinema workers about allowing pets inside

2. *[Inference]* How does the man act toward his dog's behavior in the cinema?

   - A) He tries to hide the dog under his seat

   - B) He responds calmly as if the dog were an ordinary moviegoer

   - C) He gets angry at the usherette for interrupting him

   - D) He leaves the cinema immediately before the film ends

3. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In the passage, what does the word "usherette" most likely mean?

   - A) A cinema worker who guides people to their seats

   - B) The person who sells popcorn and drinks

   - C) The actor who plays the hero in the film

   - D) The manager who owns the cinema building


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Summary: A Surprise at the Cinema** (60-80 words): Summarize the story of the man and his dog at the cinema in your own words, including the surprise ending.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Books vs. Films** (80-110 words): Do you usually prefer reading a book or watching the film made from it? Write a short paragraph explaining your opinion with at least two reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **usherette** (*kadin yer gosterici*): The usherette checked our tickets and showed us to our seats.

- **completely amazed** (*tamamen saskina donmus, hayrete dusmus*): She was completely amazed when her small dog opened the door by itself.

- **tragically** (*trajik bir sekilde, acikli bir bicimde*): The main character died tragically at the end of the movie.

- **nod (nodded)** (*basini sallayarak onaylamak*): The teacher nodded to show that she agreed with my answer.

- **to tell the truth** (*dogrusunu soylemek gerekirse*): To tell the truth, I did not enjoy the book as much as I expected.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Metindeki temel olaylari kendi cumlelerimle dogru sirayla ozetledim mi?

- [ ] Simple Past Tense (gecmis zaman) fiillerini dogru kullandim mi?

- [ ] Metinden ogrendigim yeni kelimelerden en az ucunu yazimda kullandim mi?

- [ ] Noktalama isaretlerini ve paragraf duzenini kontrol ettim mi?

## Passage 34: Tom's Diary

- **ID:** `raw1_034` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Student Life & Diary | **Sayfa:** 37
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_034_tom_s_diary.mp3` (*UK · en-GB-RyanNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. In his class, Tom is studying a play by Samuel Beckett that he finds very ____. *(İpucu: adjective)*

2. Anna is an American ____ staying at the Mayfair Hotel. *(İpucu: noun)*

3. Tom is reading a fantastic book about ____ called The Jewel in the Crown. *(İpucu: country)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What new schedule does Tom mention on Monday?

   - A) French every morning and dance at night

   - B) Dance every morning and French at night

   - C) Drama every morning and TV at night

   - D) No classes in the morning or at night

2. Where is Anna staying?

   - A) At the coffee bar

   - B) At the Bailey School

   - C) At the Mayfair Hotel

   - D) At the folk club

3. What does Tom think about the folk club he visited on Wednesday evening?

   - A) It was very boring

   - B) It was very good

   - C) It was closed for the evening

   - D) It was too expensive


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tom thinks the play Endgame is very exciting.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Tom hopes to meet Anna again after meeting her at the coffee bar.


### 2. Reading

Tom is a student at the Bailey School. This is his diary.

1996 / November

25 Monday
Everything's changing. There were no classes in the mornings. Now we have French every morning and dance at night. We're studying 'Endgame', a play by Samuel Beckett. It's very boring.

26 Tuesday
In the evening my friend and I were at the coffee bar and I have met Anna - a beautiful American nurse. She's staying at the Mayfair Hotel just near the Bailey School. I want to see Anna again!

27 Wednesday
My friend likes TV. I don't. Not every night! We were at the folk club this evening - very good. I'm reading a fantastic book about India called 'The Jewel in the Crown' by Paul Scott.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A*

1. At Tom's school there are dance classes every morning.

   - True

   - False

2. Tom enjoys studying 'Endgame' very much.

   - True

   - False

3. On Tuesday evening Tom was at the coffee bar.

   - True

   - False

4. Tom enjoys being with Anna.

   - True

   - False


*B*

5. Where is Anna from?

6. Where is the Mayfair Hotel?

7. What doesn't Tom like doing every night?

8. How was the folk club?

9. What is the name of the book Tom is reading?


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Inference]* What can we infer about Tom's interests from his diary entries?

   - A) He loves watching television every single evening

   - B) He prefers studying Samuel Beckett over reading novels

   - C) He is unhappy about meeting new people at the coffee bar

   - D) He prefers social outings and good books over watching TV every night

2. *[Main Idea]* What is this passage mainly about?

   - A) Tom's plans for a holiday in India next summer

   - B) Three days of Tom's school life and free-time activities

   - C) A review of Samuel Beckett's most famous plays

   - D) Why the Bailey School decided to change its teachers

3. *[Detail]* Why does Tom write "Everything's changing" on Monday?

   - A) His morning and evening class routine has become busier

   - B) He has stopped going to school altogether

   - C) He has moved from the Bailey School to a hotel

   - D) His French teacher has replaced all his other classes with TV


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Guided Diary Entry: Tom's Thursday** (60-80 words): Write a short diary entry for Tom's next day (Thursday, November 28th) describing his classes, an evening activity, and his reading or meeting Anna.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: Keeping a Diary** (80-110 words): Do you think keeping a diary is a good habit for students? Write a short paragraph giving your reasons.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **boring** (*sikici*): The history lecture was so boring that several students fell asleep.

- **just near** (*hemen yaninda, cok yakininda*): There is a small cafe just near the train station.

- **fantastic** (*harika, muhtesem*): She read a fantastic novel during her summer holiday.

- **play** (*tiyatro oyunu*): We are studying a famous modern play in our literature class.

- **nurse** (*hemsire*): My cousin works as a nurse at a large city hospital.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

## Passage 35: Elvis Presley

- **ID:** `raw1_035` | **Seviye:** `A2` | **Konu:** Music & Biography | **Sayfa:** 38
- **Ses Dosyası:** `../audio/raw1/raw1_035_elvis_presley.mp3` (*US · en-US-JennyNeural · Hız -8%*)

### 1. Listening (Metni Okumadan Dinleyerek Çözün)

**A. Gap-Fill (Boşluk Doldurma)**

1. Elvis had a ____ brother who died at birth. *(İpucu: noun / adjective)*

2. Of all his early jobs, the one Elvis liked most was working as a ____. *(İpucu: job)*

3. Tom Parker became Elvis's manager in ____ and turned him into a rock and roll star. *(İpucu: year)*


**B. Listening Multiple Choice**

1. What does the passage tell us about Elvis's parents?

   - A) They were wealthy business owners

   - B) They were poor, kind, and loving

   - C) They worked as famous singers

   - D) They moved to England when Elvis was young

2. How did Sam Phillips help Elvis?

   - A) He noticed that Elvis was a talented singer

   - B) He taught Elvis how to drive a lorry

   - C) He became Elvis's manager in 1955

   - D) He gave Elvis his first job at a cinema

3. What do many people say caused Elvis Presley's death?

   - A) He died in a car accident while driving a lorry

   - B) He died of old age in his eighties

   - C) He died because of a serious heart surgery in 1955

   - D) Many people believe he took too many drugs and pills


**C. True / False / Not Given**

1. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Elvis became a famous singer immediately after leaving school without working other jobs.

2. [ ] True  [ ] False  [ ] Not Given — Elvis won many gold discs during his lifetime.


### 2. Reading

Elvis Presley was born on January 8th, 1935, in East Tupelo, Mississippi. His twin brother died at birth. Elvis grew up in a poor home, but his parents, Vernon and Gladys Presley, were kind and loving.

After he left school, Elvis worked in different jobs. He worked in cinemas and supermarkets, and he worked as a lorry driver. This was the job he liked most.

Two people helped Elvis in his career. One was Sam Phillips. Sam had a record company called Sun Records. He was the first person to realise that Elvis was a good singer. The second person to help Elvis was Tom Parker. Tom became Elvis' manager in 1955 and he made him into a famous rock and roll star.

Elvis won a lot of gold discs in his life. He died on August 16th, 1977. Many people say that he died because he took too many drugs and pills.


**A. Kitaptaki Orijinal Sorular**


*A*

1. Elvis' parents were not good people.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

2. Elvis was the youngest child in the family.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

3. Elvis worked in different jobs because he wanted to earn more money.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

4. Elvis enjoyed working as a lorry driver.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

5. Sam Phillips played an important role in Elvis' career.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

6. Tom Parker was the manager of a record company called Sun Records.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information

7. Elvis died on a winter day at the age of 42.

   - True

   - False

   - No Information


**B. Ekstra Sınav Soruları (Proficiency / IELTS / TOEFL)**

1. *[Main Idea]* What is the main idea of the passage?

   - A) How rock and roll music was invented in the 1950s

   - B) The history of Sun Records and its other singers

   - C) The life and music career of Elvis Presley

   - D) Why lorry drivers often become famous musicians

2. *[Vocabulary in Context]* In paragraph 3, the word "realise" is closest in meaning to ____.

   - A) forget

   - B) understand or notice

   - C) invent

   - D) refuse to believe

3. *[Detail]* How did the roles of Sam Phillips and Tom Parker differ in Elvis's career?

   - A) Sam Phillips discovered Elvis's talent, while Tom Parker managed him to stardom

   - B) Tom Parker owned Sun Records and hired Sam Phillips as Elvis's manager

   - C) Both men worked with Elvis only after he won gold discs

   - D) Neither man believed Elvis could become a rock and roll star


### 3. Writing

- **Task 1 — Summary: The Life of Elvis Presley** (70-90 words): Write a short summary of Elvis Presley's life and career based on the passage.

- **Task 2 — Opinion Paragraph: The Price of Fame** (80-110 words): Do you think becoming famous brings more happiness or more problems to a person's life? Write a short paragraph explaining your view.


**Useful Vocabulary & Collocations:**

- **grow up** (*buyumek, yetismek*): She grew up in a small village near the sea.

- **lorry driver** (*kamyon soforu*): His uncle worked as a lorry driver for twenty years.

- **career** (*kariyer, meslek hayati*): With hard work, she built a successful career in music.

- **realise** (*fark etmek, anlamak*): He did not realise how difficult the exam would be.

- **gold disc** (*altin plak (muzik odulu)*): The band won several gold discs for their bestselling album.

- **manager** (*menajer, yonetici*): A good manager helps a singer organize concerts and contracts.


**Self-Assessment Checklist:**

- [ ] Elvis Presley'nin hayatindaki onemli tarihleri ve donum noktalarini dogru aktardim mi?

- [ ] Gecmis zaman (was born, grew up, worked, became) fiillerini dogru cekimledim mi?

- [ ] Fikirlerimi baglaclarla (after, because, but) akici sekilde bagladim mi?

- [ ] Kelime sayisi sinirina ve yazim kurallarina uydum mu?
